We show that failure is predictable early from the agent's internal representations: lightweight per-round probes on hidden activations anticipate eventual episode failure as early as the first interaction round, where scorers reading only the agent's observable behavior are barely better than chance. We turn this signal into a practical abort cascade: one distribution-free calibrated gate per round, with per-round recall budgets jointly searched so that eventually-successful episodes survive all gates at a user-specified global rate; this episode-level guarantee is the one that matters in deployment, since false-abort risk accumulates across gates. Across two agent models on TextCraft, the cascade meets every recall target from 90% to 97% and, at the 90% target, saves 47.1% +/- 10.3% (Qwen-2.5-7B) and 37.2% +/- 8.8% (Llama-3.2-3B) of inference compute, 1.6--1.7x the best single-gate policy. An otherwise-identical cascade reading only behavior saves roughly half as much, and adding behavioral features to the probe yields no further gain: the hidden states capture what behavior reveals. Finally, we characterize the sample complexity of certifying high recall targets, telling practitioners which recall promises their data can, and provably cannot, back. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [247] [RMISC: A Large-scale Real-world Multivariate Corpus for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06504)
*Qian Sun,Yong-Ming Tian,Jia-Wei Huang,Cheng Feng,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含约200个数据集、1420亿时间点的大规模真实多变量时间序列语料库RMISC，并通过实验证明：在真实多变量数据上预训练的时间序列基础模型（TSFMs），其零样本泛化能力显著优于仅在合成多变量数据上训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFMs主要使用合成多变量数据预训练，但合成数据可能无法捕捉真实时间序列中的复杂时间动态和跨变量关系。因此，需探究真实语料训练的TSFMs是否及在多大程度上优于合成数据训练的模型。

Method: 1) 构建RMISC语料库：大规模、高质量、开放获取的真实多变量时间序列档案，涵盖约200个数据集、1420亿时间点，来自多个领域。2) 在单变量、合成多变量与真实多变量三种数据上预训练四种先进TSFMs。3) 在标准分布内和分布外基准上评估零样本泛化能力。

Result: 融入真实多变量数据显著提升了单变量和多变量TSFMs的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 真实多变量数据对开发更强TSFMs具有重要贡献，为理解真实多变量数据如何助力TSFMs发展提供了深入见解。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the emergence of multivariate modeling using time series foundation models (TSFMs), which achieve advanced zero-shot generalization. Modern multivariate TSFMs are predominantly pretrained on multivariate synthetic data, which is easier to scale but may fail to capture the complex temporal dynamics and cross-variable relationships present in real-world time series. This raises a key question: Whether and to what extent the leading TSFMs trained with the real-world corpus perform better than those trained with synthetic data? To answer this, we establish the RMISC corpus, a considerably large-scale, high-quality, openly accessible, real-world, and multivariate time series archive that contains around 200 datasets and 142 billion time points across diverse domains. Furthermore, we pretrain four advanced TSFMs on univariate, synthetic multivariate, and real-world multivariate data and evaluate their zero-shot generalization capabilities on standard in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks. Experimental results show that incorporating real-world multivariate data predominantly improves the generalization performance for both univariate and multivariate TSFMs. These results provide a deeper understanding of how real-world multivariate data contributes to the development of stronger TSFMs.

</details>


### [248] [FootsiesGym: A Fighting Game Benchmark for Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06514)
*Chase McDonald,Nathan Tsang,Wesley N. Kerr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FootsiesGym是一个基于2D格斗游戏Footsies的开源强化学习环境，专门用于研究不完美信息下的零和双人博弈。它通过向量化模拟器实现高效训练，并提供了多个强化学习算法的基准测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习环境在非平凡的不完美信息博弈中要么过于复杂难以分析，要么过于简单缺乏策略深度。作者希望创建一个既保留格斗游戏核心循环对抗机制（非传递性策略交互），又足够简单以便高效分析和训练的环境。

Method: 基于HiFight的极简2D格斗游戏Footsies构建环境，提取格斗游戏中"立回"（neutral play）的循环、非传递性策略交互；提供向量化模拟器以支持标准硬件上的高吞吐量训练。

Result: 成功构建了开源环境，在标准硬件上实现了高效的向量化训练，并对多种强化学习算法进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: FootsiesGym为研究不完美信息双人零和博弈中的非传递性策略交互提供了一个可复现、易用且高效的实验平台，并指出了若干开放研究方向。

Abstract: We present FootsiesGym, an open-source environment for learning in a non-trivial two-player, zero-sum, imperfect-information game. Built on HiFight's minimalist 2D fighting game Footsies, it isolates the cyclic, non-transitive strategic interactions of fighting game neutral play while remaining simple enough for efficient analysis. We provide a vectorized simulator that enables high-throughput training on standard hardware, making the environment accessible and reproducible. We describe the design of the environment, benchmark several reinforcement learning algorithms, and discuss open research directions it enables. The code is available at https://github.com/como-research/FootsiesGym.

</details>


### [249] [FreqDepthKV: Frequency-Guided Depth Sharing for Robust KV Cache Compression in Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06519)
*Anna Córdoba,Adam Puente Tercero,Nerea Angulo Hijo,Mar Linares Tercero,Julia Barrientos,Ainhoa Miranda,Jesús Olivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FreqDepthKV 提出了一种推理时 KV 缓存压缩方法，通过将相邻层 KV 状态分解为共享低频深度分量和稀疏高频残差，并利用轻量级在线探针动态分配注意力头到三种缓存模式，在长上下文任务中以 3.9 倍有效压缩率接近全 KV 精度，同时显著提升吞吐量并降低延迟和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理受限于 KV 缓存的显存和带宽开销，现有激进压缩方法会丢失层特异性证据，影响检索和多步推理所需的信息完整性。

Method: 将相邻层 KV 状态分解为共享的低频深度分量和稀疏高频残差；设计轻量级在线探针，根据注意力头对重建敏感注意力的贡献程度分配其到共享深度、残差深度或精确缓存三种模式，使压缩策略无需重训练即可自适应提示结构。

Result: 在 32k 预填充窗口下，Exact Match 58.3、F1 63.0、ROUGE-L 32.5、pass@1 48.1；解码吞吐量 70.4 tokens/s，TTFT 2.06 秒，峰值 KV 内存 6.2 GB，实现 3.9 倍有效压缩比。

Conclusion: FreqDepthKV 在长上下文问答、检索、摘要和代码生成任务中，以显著更小的缓存预算保持任务精度，效果优于先前的压缩缓存方法，接近全 KV 缓存性能。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is increasingly limited by the memory and bandwidth cost of KV caches, yet aggressive compression can remove the layer-specific evidence needed for retrieval and multi-step reasoning. We introduce FreqDepthKV, an inference-time cache compression method that factorizes adjacent-layer KV states into shared low-frequency depth components and sparse high-frequency residuals. A lightweight online probe assigns attention heads to shared-depth, residual-depth, or exact cache modes according to their contribution to reconstruction-sensitive attention logits, allowing the compression policy to adapt to prompt structure without retraining. Across long-context question answering, needle retrieval, summarization, and code generation benchmarks, FreqDepthKV preserves task accuracy under substantially smaller cache budgets. With a 32k-token prefill window, FreqDepthKV reaches 58.3 Exact Match, 63.0 F1, 32.5 ROUGE-L, and 48.1 pass@1, closely matching full KV while outperforming prior compressed-cache methods. It also improves decoding throughput to 70.4 tokens/s, reduces TTFT to 2.06 seconds, and lowers peak KV memory to 6.2 GB, achieving a 3.9x effective compression ratio.

</details>


### [250] [Bridging Physical Reasoning and Task Generalization via Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06522)
*Han-Jun Ko,Jr-Jen Chen,Haobo Yuan,Hsin-Ying Lee,Tiancheng Shen,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VAORA通过两种互补奖励（视觉对齐奖励和视觉-动作对齐奖励）来抑制VLM在物理推理中的幻觉链式思维，并缩小推理与行为之间的差距，在PHYRE和Virtual Tool基准上实现了跨新任务和未见环境的泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在交互式物理推理中泛化能力差，存在两个关键失败模式：幻觉式链式思维推理（与物理现实矛盾）以及模型推理与行为之间的错位。

Method: 提出VAORA框架，包含两种奖励：1) 视觉对齐奖励（Visual Alignment Reward）：将VLM推理锚定到视觉上下文，独立于智能体动作；2) 视觉-动作对齐奖励（Visual-Action Alignment Reward）：将推理基于模型动作所诱导的视觉结果。使用预训练领域专家智能体估计成功概率，实现平滑、密集的奖励以提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在PHYRE和Virtual Tool基准上，VAORA在未见任务和未见环境设置下均优于基线方法，证明了奖励设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过VAORA可以诱导出基于视觉上下文的、可泛化的物理智能，为解决VLM在物理推理中的幻觉和推理-行为错位问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to generalize in interactive physical reasoning, particularly under unseen tasks and environments. Two key failure modes are prominent: hallucinated chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that contradicts physical reality, and misalignment between the model's reasoning and actions. We present VAORA (Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment), a novel reward design that directly addresses both issues. VAORA introduces two complementary rewards: Visual Alignment Reward, which anchors VLM reasoning to the visual context independent of the agent action itself, and Visual-Action Alignment Reward, which grounds reasoning in the visual outcome induced by the model's action. Together, these rewards suppress hallucinated CoT and reduce the gap between reasoning and behavior. To improve training stability, we further employ smooth, dense rewards by estimating success probabilities using a pre-trained in-domain expert agent. Experiments on PHYRE and Virtual Tool support our performances across novel-task and unseen-environment settings, confirming that grounded and generalizable physical intelligence can be induced through VAORA.

</details>


### [251] [DepthWeave-KV: Token-Adaptive Cross-Layer Residual Factorization for Long-Context KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06523)
*Anna Cordoba,Adam Puente Tercero,Nerea Angulo Hijo,Mar Linares Tercero,Julia Barrientos,Ainhoa Miranda,Jesus Olivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DepthWeave-KV 是一种令牌自适应的键值缓存压缩方法，通过跨层低秩因子分解和令牌条件深度路由器，在长上下文语言模型推理中实现接近全缓存的任务质量，达到8.3倍KV内存压缩和72.8 tokens/s的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于存储键值缓存所需的内存带宽和容量，现有压缩方法通常在层或令牌上使用统一预算，当词汇线索和语义状态需要不同保留程度时会导致检索性能下降。

Method: 1）跨深度残差因子分解：使用共享低秩通道基将键值状态分解到相邻Transformer层，保留轻量级令牌特定残差；2）令牌条件深度路由器：为指令承载和检索关键令牌分配更高重建秩；3）免校准在线误差跟踪：通过注意力输出探针在生成过程中自适应压缩，无需重训基础模型；4）融合CUDA实现：联合执行基查找、残差反量化和注意力投影以减少解码时内存流量。

Result: 在LongBench、Needle-in-a-Haystack、L-Eval及长文本QA和摘要基准上，DepthWeave-KV以显著更低内存使用达到接近全缓存的任务质量，在平均得分和检索准确率上优于此前压缩缓存方法，实现8.3倍KV内存缩减和64K上下文下72.8 tokens/s的处理速度。

Conclusion: DepthWeave-KV通过令牌自适应的跨层残差因子分解和在线误差跟踪，有效解决了长上下文推理中KV缓存的内存瓶颈，在不重训模型的情况下实现了高效的压缩推理。

Abstract: Long-context language model inference is increasingly limited by the memory bandwidth and capacity required to store key-value caches, yet existing compression methods often apply uniform budgets across layers or tokens and degrade retrieval when lexical cues and semantic states require different preservation. We introduce DepthWeave-KV, a token-adaptive cache compression method that factorizes key and value states across neighboring transformer layers using shared low-rank channel bases while retaining lightweight token-specific residuals where attention behavior is sensitive. DepthWeave-KV combines cross-depth residual factorization with a token-conditional depth router that allocates higher reconstruction rank to instruction-bearing and retrieval-critical tokens, and uses calibration-free online error tracking from attention-output probes to adapt compression during generation without retraining the base model. A fused CUDA implementation jointly performs basis lookup, residual dequantization, and attention projection to reduce decode-time memory traffic. Across LongBench, Needle-in-a-Haystack, L-Eval, and long-form QA and summarization benchmarks, DepthWeave-KV achieves near-full-cache task quality with substantially lower memory use, improving average score and retrieval accuracy over prior compressed caches while reaching 8.3x KV memory reduction and 72.8 tokens per second at 64K context.

</details>


### [252] [The Large Cancer Assistant (LCA): A Model-Agnostic Orchestration Framework for Scalable Clinical Decision Support in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06531)
*Ghassen Marrakchi,Basarab Matei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大癌症助手(LCA)，一个模型无关的后端编排框架，通过7元组架构和算法不可渗透性原则，解耦多模态数据摄入与AI推理，实现可扩展的临床决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肿瘤学多模态深度学习模型采用单体设计，将数据摄入、临床路由和AI推理刚性耦合，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 将LCA形式化为7元组架构，基于算法不可渗透性原则确保编排逻辑独立于底层黑箱AI模型；引入Entry Theory利用几何深度学习标准化多模态患者数据；通过癌症切换模块动态编排数据；输出标准化中间载荷隔离核心AI执行与医院IT基础设施。

Result: 概念验证在四个技术场景中验证了编排逻辑：名义流程执行开销可忽略；AI模型替换时保持路由投影不变，证明了算法不可渗透性；注入数据异常时实现100%召回率生成补充数据请求；多协议执行能力成功验证。

Conclusion: 通过结构化解耦多模态摄入与特征推理，LCA提供了高度可适应和模块化的编排基础。标准化中间载荷建立了清晰的架构边界，为下游电子病历互操作性奠定基础。

Abstract: - Objective: Multimodal deep learning models in oncology are currently limited by monolithic designs that rigidly couple data ingestion, clinical routing, and artificial intelligence (AI) inference. To address this inflexibility, we propose the Large Cancer Assistant (LCA), a model-agnostic, post-hoc orchestration framework designed for scalable clinical decision support. - Methods: The LCA is mathematically formalized as a 7-tuple architecture grounded in the principle of Algorithmic Impermeability, ensuring the orchestration logic remains strictly independent of underlying black-box AI models. We introduce the Entry Theory, leveraging Geometric Deep Learning (GDL) to standardize multimodal patient data along distinct structural and medical axes. The system dynamically orchestrates data via a Cancer Switching Module and intentionally isolates the core AI execution from volatile hospital IT infrastructures by outputting a Standardized Intermediate Payload (SIP). - Results: A Proof of Concept (PoC) validated the orchestration logic across four technical scenarios. The framework executed a nominal flow with negligible orchestration overhead. It empirically demonstrated algorithmic impermeability by maintaining an invariant routing projection during AI model swaps, and it validated strict failure-safety by achieving a 100\% recall rate in generating targeted Supplementary Data Requests (SDR) under injected data anomalies. Multi-protocol execution capability was also successfully verified. - Conclusion: By structurally decoupling multimodal ingestion from feature inference, the LCA provides a highly adaptable and modular orchestration foundation. The SIP establishes a clear architectural boundary, natively setting the stage for downstream Electronic Medical Record (EMR) interoperability as an independent future paradigm.

</details>


### [253] [Rethinking Indic AI from a Lens of Cultural Heritage Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06544)
*Aparna Madva,Sharath Srivatsa,Srinath Srinivasa,Tulika Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了印度NLP的发展历程，探讨了AI对印度语言文化的影响，分析了印地语系的结构与社会语言学挑战，并提出了基于诠释学推理的"文化感知"研究方向，旨在构建更包容的印地基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI如何影响印度的语言和文化基础，解决AI"双刃剑"带来的包容性与同质化问题。

Method: 纵向综述NLP技术演变，分析印地语言的结构与 sociolinguistic 特征，评估印地基础模型的表现，提出"文化感知"研究方向。

Result: 揭示了印地语言的丰富形态、复杂文字语法、双言现象和方言变异等挑战，指出了现有基础模型在资源与表征上的差距。

Conclusion: "文化感知"方向通过诠释学推理重新构想AI，致力于解决低资源语言公平性和文化意义输出问题，为下一代印地NLP指明方向。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI) makes inroads into different parts of the Indian subcontinent, there is significant interest in studying how AI impacts the linguistic and cultural foundations of this civilization. AI is seen as a ''double-edged sword'' where on the one hand, it can enable access and inclusion for a large population, on the other, it can homogenize worldviews and exclude underrepresented languages and worldviews. In this paper, we try to characterize this problem by addressing the extensive characteristic nature of Indian linguistics and the way they closely connect to cultural practices and worldview. We then perform a longitudinal survey of how Natural Language Processing (NLP) techniques have evolved in this space, tracing the historical development of Indic NLP, covering key milestones, methodological shifts, and resource creation efforts. In addition, the paper also examines the structural and sociolinguistic characteristics of Indian languages, such as rich morphology, complex scripts and grammar rules, diglossia, and large dialectal variation, and explains how these create unique challenges for building AI foundation models. We then discuss the growing role of Indic foundation models and analyze how these models address these long-standing resource and representation gaps. Finally, we propose a research direction called 'Culture Sensing', which re-imagines AI based on hermeneutic reasoning. Culture Sensing aims to address open problems such as ensuring equitable performance across low-resource languages and producing outputs that are culturally meaningful. By bringing together past work, current techniques, and emerging trends, this paper outlines research directions that can guide the next phase of Indic NLP and contribute to the development of more robust and inclusive Indic foundation models.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [TMF-RSE: Tri-Modal Fusion with Regional Semantics and Evidential Uncertainty for Lung Severity Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06356)
*Fadi Abdeladhim Zidi,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdellah Zakaria Sellam,Gaby Maroun,Fadi Dornaika,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种三模态深度学习框架TMF-RSE，结合外观特征、结构特征和视觉-语言模型语义特征，通过互补融合机制和证据回归进行肺部疾病严重程度量化，在两个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从胸部影像中准确量化肺部疾病严重程度对于临床决策和资源分配至关重要，现有方法未能充分融合多模态信息。

Method: 提出TMF-RSE框架，包含三模态融合：2D胸部影像的外观特征、肺部分割掩膜的结构特征、视觉-语言模型的语义特征；采用互补融合机制整合语义引导、结构先验和跨模态层次交互；使用证据回归同时输出严重程度预测和不确定性估计。

Result: 在Per-COVID-19数据集上MAE为4.02、Pearson相关系数为0.9629；在RALO地理范围评估上MAE为0.339、PC为0.973，优于近期基于Transformer的基线方法。

Conclusion: TMF-RSE通过三模态互补融合和证据回归有效提升了肺部疾病严重程度量化的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate quantification of lung disease severity from chest imaging is critical for clinical decision-making and resource allocation. We propose a tri-modal deep learning framework, TMF-RSE (Tri-Modal Fusion with Regional Semantics and Evidential Uncertainty), that combines appearance features from two-dimensional chest inputs, structural features from lung segmentation masks, and semantic features from vision-language models (VLMs) for severity quantification. Our approach employs complementary fusion mechanisms that integrate semantic guidance, structural priors, and hierarchical interactions across modalities. The model employs evidential regression to provide both severity predictions and uncertainty estimates. Experiments on the Per-COVID-19 CT and RALO datasets show that TMF-RSE outperforms recent transformer-based baselines, achieving MAE of 4.02 and Pearson correlation of 0.9629 on Per-COVID-19 validation, and 0.339 MAE / 0.973 PC on RALO geographic extent.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Quaternion-Averaging-Based Adaptive Complementary Filter for Pedestrian Dead Reckoning With a Foot-Mounted AHRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05451)
*Shunsei Yamagishi,Lei Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四元数平均的自适应互补滤波器（QAACF），用于足部安装AHRS的行人航位推算（PDR），在保持较低计算成本的同时提高了姿态估计精度。该方法通过Markley四元数平均融合角速度、加速度和磁力计数据，并根据步态相位和磁干扰水平自适应调整权重，实验结果表明其均方根误差低于现有滤波器，计算成本低于卡尔曼滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPS在室内和城市峡谷环境中信号衰减严重，而PDR技术不受此影响。然而，足部安装AHRS的PDR精度依赖于姿态估计算法的精度，现有方法在精度和计算成本之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出Quaternion-Averaging-Based Adaptive Complementary Filter (QAACF)，核心创新包括：(1) 使用Markley四元数平均替代线性插值，更严谨地融合角速度、加速度和磁力计导出的四元数；(2) 根据步态相位（检测零速区间）和磁干扰水平自适应调整各传感器权重。

Result: 实验表明，QAACF在姿态估计上取得了较低的均方根误差（RMSE），优于现有姿态估计滤波器，同时计算成本显著低于卡尔曼滤波器。

Conclusion: QAACF在精度和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，适用于足部安装PDR系统的姿态估计，特别适合资源受限的室内导航应用场景。

Abstract: Pedestrian Dead Reckoning (PDR) can be applied to indoor navigation systems. GPS suffers from signal degradation due to roofs and high-rise buildings, whereas PDR can estimate positions without being affected by such signal degradation. The accuracy of a foot-mounted AHRS(Attitude and Heading Reference System)-based PDR depends on the accuracy of the attitude estimation algorithm used in the AHRS. In this article, a Quaternion-Averaging-Based Adaptive Complementary Filter (QAACF) for PDR with a foot-mounted AHRS is proposed to improve estimation accuracy while reducing computational cost. QAACF fuses a quaternion derived from angular velocity with quaternions derived from acceleration and magnetic field measurements using Markley's quaternion averaging, which combines two quaternions more rigorously than linear interpolation. In addition, QAACF adaptively adjusts the weights of angular velocity, acceleration, and magnetic field measurements according to gait phases and the level of magnetic disturbances. Experimental results showed that the proposed QAACF achieves low Root Mean Square Errors (RMSEs) compared to existing attitude estimation filters while requiring lower computational cost than Kalman filters.

</details>


### [256] [Learning 4D Geometric Priors for Inference-Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05468)
*Jianjun Zhang,Jian Zhu,Taiyi Su,Chong Ma,Zitai Huang,Yi Xu,Hanli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MECo-WAM 通过多专家协同训练，在视频-动作表征中注入与动作相关的4D几何先验，同时保持轻量推理图，在LIBERO（98.2%）、RoboTwin 2.0（92.6%）和真实世界任务中提升操作性能且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-动作联合训练方法主要优化面向外观的视频潜变量，未能充分捕捉随时间演变的几何结构，而这对于精确操作至关重要。

Method: 提出MECo-WAM，结合视频专家和动作专家，引入轻量4D专家（由冻结的VGGT编码器监督）；采用非对称专家可见性防止非因果捷径；提出衰减4D读掩码注意力在训练早期提供几何引导并逐步移除；提出动作感知时间几何蒸馏对齐帧内几何关系和时间演变，并关注动作相关区域。

Result: LIBERO达98.2%，RoboTwin 2.0达92.6%，在真实世界操作任务中均优于基线，且推理阶段无额外计算开销。

Conclusion: MECo-WAM有效将4D几何先验注入视频-动作表征，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升操作性能，验证了多专家协同训练和渐进式几何蒸馏的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation by jointly modeling visual future dynamics and executable action sequences. However, existing video-action co-training methods primarily optimize appearance-oriented video latents, which may insufficiently capture the temporally evolving geometry required for precise manipulation. We propose MECo-WAM, a Multi-Expert Co-Training World Action Model that injects action-relevant 4D geometric priors into video-action representations while preserving the original lightweight inference graph. During training, MECo-WAM combines video and action experts with a lightweight 4D expert supervised by relational targets from a frozen VGGT encoder. Asymmetric expert visibility prevents non-causal shortcuts from auxiliary geometry to action generation. To transfer geometric knowledge into the deployed video-action pathway, we introduce decayed 4D read-mask attention, which provides restricted current-frame geometric guidance early in training and progressively removes this dependency. We further propose action-aware temporal geometric distillation, which aligns within-frame geometric relations and their temporal evolution while emphasizing visual regions most relevant to robot actions. At deployment, all auxiliary 4D components are removed. Experiments on LIBERO (98.2%), RoboTwin 2.0 (92.6%), and challenging real-world manipulation tasks show that MECo-WAM improves manipulation performance without increasing inference cost.

</details>


### [257] [GEM-Occ: From Visual Geometry Evidence to Embodied Semantic Occupancy Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05543)
*Hu Zhu,Bohan Li,Xianda Guo,Hongsi Liu,Baorui Peng,Mingqi Yuan,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HIOcc层次化室内占用基准和GEM-Occ高斯证据记忆框架，统一了多个室内数据集，支持局部预测、房间级在线建图到建筑级跨场景映射，通过将局部视觉几何作为瞬态证据融合到持久化层次记忆中，显著提升了室内语义占用建图的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内占用基准和方法主要关注单视图预测或房间级在线感知，缺乏对跨连接室内空间的长时序语义建图的探索。

Method: 提出GEM-Occ框架，将局部视觉几何预测作为瞬态证据，转换为语义高斯占用证据和自由空间射线证据，通过可见性和不确定性感知的因果更新融合到持久化层次记忆中（本地缓存-房间子图-建筑级图），并支持通过高斯到占用喷溅进行查询。

Result: 在HIOcc基准上的实验表明，GEM-Occ在局部占用预测、在线地图稳定性、自由空间推理、回访一致性和建筑级可扩展性上均优于先前的室内占用和高斯建图基线。

Conclusion: GEM-Occ通过证据记忆框架有效解决了长时序语义占用建图难题，为室内智能体提供了可扩展的结构化空间记忆。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy provides a structured spatial memory for embodied indoor agents by jointly representing occupied regions, observed free space, unknown areas, and object semantics. However, existing indoor occupancy benchmarks and methods mainly focus on single-view prediction or room-level online perception, leaving long-horizon semantic mapping across connected indoor spaces underexplored. We introduce HIOcc, a hierarchical indoor occupancy benchmark that unifies ScanNet, ScanNet++, and Matterport3D under a common sparse semantic occupancy format while preserving their native observation geometries, including perspective RGB-D frames and pano-centric observation groups. HIOcc supports three complementary evaluation regimes: local semantic occupancy prediction, room-level online occupancy mapping, and building-level mapping across connected panoramic environments. We further propose GEM-Occ, a Gaussian Evidence Memory framework for semantic occupancy mapping. Rather than using pointmaps as persistent map states, GEM-Occ treats local visual geometry predictions as transient evidence, converts them into semantic Gaussian occupancy evidence and free-space ray evidence, and fuses them into a persistent hierarchical memory through visibility- and uncertainty-aware causal updates. The memory is organized into local caches, room-level submaps, and a building-level graph, and can be queried at any time through Gaussian-to-occupancy splatting. Experiments on HIOcc show that GEM-Occ improves local occupancy prediction, online map stability, free-space reasoning, revisit consistency, and building-level scalability over prior indoor occupancy and Gaussian-based mapping baselines.

</details>


### [258] [Dynamic Evaluation of Classical and Control-Aware Optimal Trajectory Planning in Robot Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05544)
*Bhanuka Dayawansa,Rohan Munasinghe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种控制感知的最优轨迹规划框架，通过显式融入机械臂动力学和控制代价，在有限时域内优化轨迹生成。相比经典的纯运动学规划器（三次、五次、梯形），该方法能显著降低跟踪误差、修正力矩和闭环执行成本，证明了运动学平滑性并不等同于动力学执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轨迹规划器虽简单平滑，但纯运动学方法忽略了系统动力学和控制代价，导致名义上的平滑轨迹在实际执行中产生非线性跟踪误差和过大的修正控制作用，需要一种控制感知的轨迹规划方法来桥接规划与控制之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出控制感知最优轨迹规划框架，在有限时域内显式融入机械臂动力学和执行器代价；引入中点线性化策略提升大范围点对点运动的逼近精度；构建统一评估框架，在相同非线性动力学、控制器结构和执行器约束下对所有规划器进行公平对比。

Result: 在非线性简化UR5机械臂仿真中，所提方法相比经典规划器在所有测试场景下持续降低跟踪误差、修正力矩和闭环执行成本，实现了执行器代价和执行成本的大幅缩减。

Conclusion: 运动学平滑性并不保证动力学执行效率；将控制代价和系统动力学显式纳入轨迹规划可以显著提升实际执行性能。

Abstract: Trajectory planning strongly influences tracking accuracy, actuator demand, and overall execution behavior in robotic manipulators. Classical planners such as cubic, quintic, and trapezoidal profiles are widely used for their simplicity and smoothness, yet they remain purely kinematic and ignore system dynamics and control effort during trajectory generation. As a result, nominally smooth trajectories can lead to inefficient nonlinear execution and increased corrective control action. This paper presents a control-aware optimal trajectory planning framework that explicitly incorporates manipulator dynamics and actuator effort within a finite-horizon formulation. A midpoint linearization strategy is introduced to improve approximation accuracy for large point-to-point motions. In contrast to prior comparisons, the proposed approach enables fair, isolated evaluation of trajectory generation effects under identical closed-loop nonlinear execution conditions. To this end, a unified evaluation framework is developed in which all planners are executed under identical nonlinear dynamics, controller structure, and actuator constraints. Simulations on a nonlinear simplified UR5 manipulator show that the proposed approach consistently reduces tracking error, corrective torque, and closed-loop execution cost compared to classical methods, achieving substantial reductions in actuator effort and execution cost across all evaluated scenarios, demonstrating that kinematic smoothness alone does not ensure dynamically efficient execution.

</details>


### [259] [Physics-Regularized Machine Learning for Proprioceptive Vehicle Localization Using Onboard Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05663)
*Abinav Kalyanasundaram,Karthikeyan Chandra Sekaran,Wolfgang Utschick,Michael Botsch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRML2混合框架，通过可微分卡尔曼滤波实现物理正则化的端到端学习，结合IMU与车载传感器进行车辆定位，在公开数据集上实现高精度实时定位，并引入低摩擦条件新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMU与卫星融合定位在信号中断时性能严重下降，而机器学习虽能提升IMU自主定位但未充分利用量产车现有传感器，需融合物理模型与数据驱动方法以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出PRML2框架，将机器学习模型通过可微分卡尔曼滤波器进行端到端训练，实现物理正则化学习，融合IMU与车载传感器进行车辆位姿估计。

Result: 在公开数据集上PRML2实现了优越的定位精度，并展示了实时运行能力；同时发布了低摩擦条件下的新数据集。

Conclusion: 通过将学习与物理先验相结合，PRML2为退化传感条件下的车辆定位提供了鲁棒且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and robust localization is essential for autonomous mobility systems in real-world environments. While fusing Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) data with satellite-based correction signals provides precise vehicle pose estimates, performance degrades substantially during outages. Recent studies indicate that Machine Learning (ML) can improve IMU-based proprioceptive localization, highlighting untapped potential for onboard sensors readily available in production vehicles. This paper introduces Physics-Regularized Machine Learning for Localization (PRML2), a hybrid framework that combines the complementary strengths of Kalman filtering and data-driven learning to estimate vehicle pose directly from onboard sensors. A key aspect of PRML2 is its physics-regularized learning, enabled by end-to-end training of an ML model through a differentiable Kalman filter. This improves consistency with vehicle motion models, thereby enhancing both localization accuracy and generalization across driving conditions. We evaluate the performance limits of ML-enhanced onboard odometry on a publicly available dataset and show that PRML2 achieves superior localization accuracy and demonstrates real-time capability. This work also introduces a novel dataset to support vehicle localization research under low-friction conditions. The proposed framework provides a robust and cost-effective solution for vehicle localization under degraded sensing conditions by integrating learning with physics-based priors.

</details>


### [260] [Efficient Transfer Learning of Robot Dynamic Models Using Morphological Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05665)
*Pavlo Kupyn,Yuya Hamamatsu,Roza Gkliva,Asko Ristolainen,Maarja Kruusmaa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的迁移学习框架，通过自编码器域自适应方法，将在大型软体鳍驱动水下机器人上训练的动力学模型迁移至形态相似但尺度更小的机器人，在无标签数据条件下实现了准确的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决形态相似但尺度和水动力学特性不同的软体水下机器人之间动力学模型迁移困难的问题，减少对小目标机器人进行大量标注数据采集的成本。

Method: 采用神经网络迁移学习框架，结合自编码器域自适应方法，学习源域（大型机器人）和目标域（小型机器人）之间的共享潜空间表征，实现跨机器人动力学对齐与状态估计。

Result: 在两种真实水下机器人上的实验表明，所提方法能够在目标平台无标签数据条件下准确估计体坐标系速度，实现高效的跨机器人动力学迁移。

Conclusion: 本文所提出的迁移学习框架有效实现了形态相似软体水下机器人之间的无监督动力学模型迁移，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This study proposes a neural network-based transfer learning framework for modeling the dynamics of soft, fin-actuated underwater robots. We focus on morphologically similar robots that differ in scale and hydrodynamic properties. A model trained on data from a larger robot (source domain) is adapted to a smaller one (target domain) with limited labeled data. To enable label-efficient transfer, we develop an autoencoder-based domain adaptation approach that learns a shared latent representation aligning the dynamics of both robots. Experiments on two real underwater robots show that the proposed method enables accurate state estimation of the body-frame velocities on a target platform without labeled data, highlighting its potential for efficient cross-robot dynamics transfer among morphologically similar platforms.

</details>


### [261] [Uncertainty-Aware Velocity Correction for Proprioceptive Vehicle Localization using Evidential Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05669)
*Abinav Kalyanasundaram,Karthikeyan Chandra Sekaran,Wolfgang Utschick,Michael Botsch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EVC-Mamba是一种基于Mamba的学习架构，将车载传感器数据转化为虚拟速度传感器用于IMU漂移校正，无需额外硬件，在GNSS失效场景下实现接近专用传感器的定位精度（差距在10%以内），并支持40Hz边缘设备实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS失效环境下智能车辆定位可靠性问题突出，惯性导航系统漂移累积无法消除；现有方法依赖专用基础设施、昂贵外部传感器或复杂多传感器融合，部署障碍大。

Method: 采用Mamba（选择性状态空间模型）捕捉车辆运动时序动态，结合基于Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布的深度证据学习进行不确定性量化，将不确定性感知的速度估计作为虚拟修正量输入误差状态扩展卡尔曼滤波器（ES-EKF）以减少位置漂移。

Result: 在真实车辆数据上，该方法在不同GNSS失效时长下定位精度达到专用外部速度传感器的90%以内，且能以40Hz在边缘硬件上实时运行。

Conclusion: 所提架构无需额外硬件即可实现可靠的GNSS失效场景定位，支持实际边缘部署，为智能车辆长时间GNSS中断下的定位提供实用方案。

Abstract: Reliable localization in GNSS-denied environments remains a fundamental challenge for intelligent vehicles, as inertial navigation systems accumulate unbounded drift without external correction. Existing approaches provide drift correction through dedicated infrastructure, expensive external sensors, or complex multi-sensor fusion, each introducing practical deployment barriers. We propose Evidential Velocity Correction using Mamba (EVC-Mamba), a learning-based architecture that transforms onboard vehicle sensor data into a virtual velocity sensor for IMU drift correction without additional hardware. A Mamba-based selective state space model captures the temporal dynamics of vehicle motion, while evidential deep learning with a Normal-Inverse-Gamma distribution provides principled uncertainty quantification. The resulting uncertainty-aware velocity estimate is incorporated as a virtual correction measurement into an Error-State Extended Kalman Filter to reduce position drift. Evaluation on real-world vehicle data demonstrates that inertial navigation using the proposed velocity correction achieves localization accuracy within 10% of a dedicated external velocity sensor across different outage durations. The proposed architecture supports real-time onboard deployment at 40 Hz on edge hardware, enabling reliable localization during prolonged GNSS outages.

</details>


### [262] [IMR: Iterative Mode-World Weighted Regression for Multi-Agent Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05705)
*Honglin Wang,Shiyao Pan,Yun-Fu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模式加权回归损失和迭代解码器的多智能体运动预测方法，在Argoverse 2基准测试中取得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预测和基于锚点的方法分别在模态多样性和预测精度上存在局限，可能导致自动驾驶车辆的安全评估不足和行为偏差。

Method: 提出模式加权回归损失（mode-world weighted regression loss）以弥合预测与锚点方法之间的差距，缓解模式坍塌并提升世界排名和top-1置信度；同时提出迭代解码器，通过循环分段生成轨迹来提高预测精度。

Result: 所提方法在Argoverse 2多智能体运动预测基准测试中排名第一。

Conclusion: 该方法通过创新的损失函数和解码器设计，有效解决了多智能体运动预测中模态多样性与预测精度之间的平衡问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent motion prediction is essential for automated vehicles to understand the intentions of surrounding vehicles. However, previous prediction-based and anchor-based methods have limitations in mode diversity and prediction accuracy, respectively. These limitations may cause inadequate safety assessments and behavioral deviations in automated vehicles. To address this issue, a mode-world weighted regression loss is proposed to bridge the gap between these features. Specifically, this approach mitigates mode collapse while simultaneously improving world ranking and top-1 confidence. Furthermore, the proposed iterative decoder improves prediction accuracy by recurrently and segmentally generating trajectories. Experimental results show the proposed method ranks first in the Argoverse 2 multi-agent motion forecasting benchmark against other methods.

</details>


### [263] [Co-STAR: Cognitive Stimulation Therapy by an Autonomous Robot for Dementia -- A One-Week In-Home Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05709)
*Emmanuel Akinrintoyo,Nicole Salomons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发并部署了一款社交机器人在家中自主提供认知刺激疗法（CST）。9名痴呆症患者参与了一周的研究，依从率高于传统护理者主导的CST，证明了社交辅助机器人在家庭认知治疗中的可行性和潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知疗法已被证明能提升痴呆症患者的生活质量，但由于缺乏专业人员和照护者时间精力有限，其应用受到限制。为弥补这一缺口，研究者开发了可自主提供CST的社交机器人。

Method: 开发了一款能在家中自主提供认知刺激疗法的社交机器人，并对9名痴呆症患者进行了为期7天的日常机器人引导会话研究。

Result: 参与者积极与系统互动，完成了近一半的计划会话，依从率高于传统照护者主导的CST。家庭成员在启动会话和参与活动中发挥了关键作用。

Conclusion: 社交辅助机器人在家中提供认知治疗具有可行性和潜力，为扩大痴呆症护理的可及性提供了一种可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Cognitive therapies have been shown to enhance the quality of life and well-being of people living with dementia (PwDs). However, their use remains limited due to a shortage of trained professionals and the significant time and training required of informal caregivers. To address this gap, we developed and deployed a social robot capable of autonomously delivering cognitive stimulation therapy (CST) in the home. Nine PwDs participated in a one-week ($7$ days) study that involved daily robot-led sessions. Participants engaged positively with the system, completing nearly half of the scheduled sessions, an adherence rate higher than typically observed in caregiver-led CST. Our findings also highlight the crucial role of family members, who often supported session initiation and occasionally joined the activities, enriching the interactions. This work demonstrates the feasibility and potential of socially assistive robots to deliver in-home cognitive therapy, offering a scalable approach to extend access to dementia care.

</details>


### [264] [Observation Quality Matters: Robust Multi-Fisheye Calibration via Failure-Oriented Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05777)
*Peize Liu,Zhe Tong,Chen Feng,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过故障导向分析揭示多鱼眼相机系统标定失败的主因是内参初始化问题（径向跨度有限导致焦距与投影形状参数耦合），并提出CO-Calib框架——结合鲁棒学习型目标检测器与错误分析引导的帧选择器，构建初始化友好的锚点、共视多相机约束和覆盖补全帧，无需修改现有标定流程即可将标定成功率从68.1%提升至99.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多鱼眼相机系统标定严重依赖经验性采集规则和观测质量，现有方法虽能联合优化内外参，但未能充分解释标定失败的根源，亟需系统性的故障分析并提出通用解决方案。

Method: 1) 故障分析揭示主导失败因素为内参初始化阶段径向跨度有限导致的焦距与鱼眼投影形状参数耦合。2) 提出CO-Calib框架：鲁棒学习型目标检测器 + 错误分析引导的帧选择器，构建初始化友好锚点、共视多相机约束和覆盖补全帧，不改变现有标定流程和优化后端。

Result: 在合成和真实多鱼眼系统上，标定成功率从68.1%提升至99.3%，外参精度提升，真实场景标定稳定性增强。

Conclusion: 多鱼眼相机标定故障的主因是内参初始化阶段的径向跨度受限问题，CO-Calib通过构建高质量数据框架可有效克服该问题，无需修改现有标定算法。

Abstract: Reliable calibration of multi-fisheye camera systems remains challenging as rig size, camera arrangement diversity, and field of view increase. Existing pipelines can jointly optimize intrinsics, extrinsics, and target poses, but their success still depends heavily on empirical capture rules and the quality of the observations supplied to the solver. This paper studies this dependency through a failure-oriented analysis. We reveal that calibration failures are not sufficiently explained by detector recall loss or global image-plane distribution imbalance. Instead, the dominant failure factor lies in intrinsic initialization: observations with limited radial span couple focal scale with fisheye projection-shape parameters, producing ill-conditioned updates. Guided by this insight, we propose CO-Calib, a plug-in calibration-data construction framework that combines a robust learning-based target detector with an error-analysis-guided frame selector. CO-Calib constructs initialization-friendly anchors, co-visible multi-camera constraints, and coverage-completion frames without changing the existing calibration workflow or optimization backend. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real multi-fisheye systems demonstrate that CO-Calib improves the overall success rate from 68.1% to 99.3%, increases extrinsic accuracy, and augments real-world calibration stability. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/HKUST-Aerial-Robotics/CO-Calib.

</details>


### [265] [FORGE: Towards Functional Tool-Use Generalization via Keypoint Trajectory Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05780)
*Chuhao Zhou,Liquan Wang,Shuxin Cao,Xiangyu Chen,Yuxuan Hu,Boyu Ma,Animesh Garg,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FORGE框架，通过解耦功能推理与动作执行，利用关键点轨迹作为中间表征，实现机器人对未见过工具的泛化打击功能，在模拟和真实环境中成功率提升超2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能轻松将书本、石头等新物体用作锤子，但机器人训练过的工具种类有限，无法将功能泛化到新工具上。现有方法在视觉上能识别工具的功能意图，却无法将这种感知相似性映射到动作空间，因为不同工具需要完全不同的运动模式。

Method: 提出FORGE两阶段策略：首先从无动作数据中预测可泛化的关键点轨迹（功能推理），然后用少量演示将这些轨迹接地为机器人动作（动作执行）。中间表征对比了可供性图像、人类视频提示和2D关键点轨迹，发现关键点轨迹在功能表达力和动作可接地性之间达到最佳平衡。

Result: 在七种工具的打击功能基准测试中，FORGE在模拟和真实环境中均显著优于现有方法，在未见过的工具上平均成功率提升超过2倍。

Conclusion: 解耦功能推理与动作执行，并采用关键点轨迹作为中间表征，是实现在新工具上功能泛化的有效路径。

Abstract: While humans readily repurpose a book, a stone, or a shoe to drive a nail, robots trained on specific tools fail to transfer the same function to novel ones -- a gap we formalize as functional generalization. Such tools share a common functional intent that is visually recognizable, yet this perceptual similarity does not carry over to action space, where each tool demands an entirely different motor pattern. To bridge this gap, we explore intermediate representations including affordance images, human video prompts, and 2D keypoint trajectories, finding that keypoint trajectories best balance functional expressiveness and action groundability. Building on this, we propose FunctiOnal Reasoning and Grounded Execution (FORGE), a two-stage policy that decouples functional reasoning from action execution: predicting generalizable keypoint trajectories from action-free data, then grounding them into robot actions with limited demonstrations. On a seven-tool hitting-function benchmark, FORGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on unseen tools in both simulation and the real world, achieving over 2X improvement in average success rate.

</details>


### [266] [GraspIT: A Dataset Bridging the Sim-to-Real gap and back for Validated Grasping SE(3) Pose Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05869)
*Paul Koch. Adem Karakurt,André Sers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspIT是一个用于鲁棒机器人抓取的综合数据集，包含约316k个RGB-D帧（1035个仿真场景+100个真实场景），通过物理滑移测试提供连续抓取质量评分，并建立了仿真与现实之间的循环验证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集无法同时提供逼真的RGB-D观测、物理验证的抓取质量标注以及仿真与现实之间的桥接，这限制了机器人对新颖物体的鲁棒抓取能力。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中构建桌面场景，通过四阶段物理滑移测试对Franka Panda平行夹爪的抓取进行标注，生成轨迹可达性检查和连续质量分数；并设计Real↔Sim循环将标注反向投影到100个真实场景中。

Result: 约230万个抓取候选中，83%通过物理测试被评为好（s≥0.50），17%虽通过力闭合但未通过滑移测试，形成分级硬负样本；最终发布包含1035个仿真场景和100个真实场景的约316k个标注RGB-D帧集，附带实例掩码、6-DoF位姿、物理属性及评分抓取。

Conclusion: GraspIT提供了超越传统力闭合评估的物理验证抓取标注和仿真-真实桥接机制，其轨迹规划功能还可用于桌面操作策略学习和行为克隆的高分辨率演示流生成。

Abstract: Robust robotic grasping of novel objects requires datasets that simultaneously provide photorealistic RGB-D observations, physically validated grasp quality annotations, and a principled bridge between simulation and the real world, which existing datasets lack to provide jointly. \textbf{GraspIT} addresses this gap: tabletop scenes in NVIDIA Isaac Sim are annotated via a four-stage physical slip-test on parallel Franka Panda instances, producing trajectory-reachability checks and continuous quality scores beyond force-closure.Of ${\sim}$2.3M candidates, 83% pass as \emph{good} ($s{\geq}0.50$); the 17% that passed force-closure but failed the slip-test provide graded hard negatives. A Real$\leftrightarrow$Sim loop back-projects these labels onto 100 real-world scenes. The release provides ${\sim}$316k annotated RGBD frame sets across 1035 sim and 100 real scenes, with instance masks, 6-DoF poses, physical object properties, and scored 6-DoF grasps. All tools are open-source and Docker-containerized. The trajectory planning within Isaac Sim further allows streaming of high resolution demonstrations for tabletop manipulation policy learning and behavior cloning.

</details>


### [267] [DexTele: A Dual-Arm Dexterous Teleoperation System Based on Motion Retargeting and Adaptive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05883)
*Yuanchuan Lai,Qing Gao,Ziyan Liang,Xianfeng Cheng,Junjie Hu,Zhaojie Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DexTele的双臂灵巧遥操作系统，通过基于视觉的运动重定向和自适应力控制，实现了跨平台精确运动映射和柔顺抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧遥操作中，运动重定向的跨平台泛化能力和抓取的交互性至关重要，但机器人架构的异构性和抓取物体的多样性给实现精确运动重定向和柔顺抓取带来了显著挑战。

Method: 1) 基于视觉的运动重定向模块：提出运动-图编码器和潜在优化方法，从人体图像生成机器人运动，实现精确便捷的跨平台运动重定向。2) 自适应抓取模块：结合视觉语言模型（VLM）与模型预测控制（MPC），预测目标物体所需抓取力并进行基于梯度的在线优化，实现柔顺抓取。

Result: 大量实验表明，DexTele实现了精确的运动重定向和柔顺抓取，并能在多个机器人平台上泛化。

Conclusion: DexTele有效解决了双臂灵巧遥操作中跨平台运动重定向和柔顺抓取的挑战，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: In dual-arm dexterous teleoperation, cross-platform generalization of motion retargeting and interactivity of grasping are crucial. However, the heterogeneity of robotic architectures and the wide variety of grasping objects pose significant challenges to achieving precise motion retargeting and compliant grasping in dual-arm dexterous teleoperation. To address these challenges, a dual-arm dexterous teleoperation system (DexTele) is proposed based on motion retargeting and adaptive force control. First, a vision-based motion retargeting module is designed to generate preliminary robot motions from human images. In this module, a motion-graph encoder and latent optimization are proposed for precise and convenient cross-platform motion retargeting. Second, an adaptive grasping module is designed to achieve compliant grasping. This module combines a vision-language model (VLM) with model predictive control (MPC), allowing the system to predict the required grasping force for a target object and perform gradient-based online optimization. Finally, extensive experiments demonstrate that the DexTele achieves precise motion retargeting and compliant grasping with generalization across multiple robot platforms.

</details>


### [268] [Intercepting an Agile Target with Net-Carrying Drones using Competitive Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05939)
*Timothée Gavin,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用携带捕捉网的敏捷无人机团队拦截敌方敏捷无人机的方法，通过MAPPO+PFSP算法进行多智能体强化学习训练，在高速模拟器中实现低层级控制，相比启发式基线方法在捕获率、捕获时间和坠毁率方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决敏捷无人机拦截任务中对手策略动态变化导致的非平稳性和灾难性遗忘问题，以及现有方法难以适应不同对手策略的局限性。

Method: 将问题建模为竞争性多智能体强化学习任务，采用MAPPO结合优先级虚构自对弈（PFSP）训练追捕者和逃逸者，使用低层级控制指令（集体推力和机体角速度）在高保真模拟器中实现敏捷飞行。

Result: 所提方法在捕获率、捕获时间和坠毁率上均优于启发式基线；消融实验证明PFSP提升了策略鲁棒性，低层级控制对学习高性能策略至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法能够训练出适应不同对手策略的鲁棒策略，并使追捕者涌现出协作战术行为。

Abstract: This article presents a solution to intercept an agile drone by a team of agile drone carrying catching nets. We formulate the problem as a competitive Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) task. To address the problem of nonstationarity and catastrophic forgetting of agents overfitting to the current opponent strategy, we train the pursuers and the evader using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) with Prioritized Fictitious Self Play (PFSP). We train the agents in a high-fidelity simulator using low-level control commands, collective thrust and body rates (CTBR), to achieve agile flights for both the pursuers and the evader. We compare the performance of the trained policies in terms of catch rate, time to catch and crash rates, against heuristic baselines and show that our solution outperforms them. Ablation studies show that PFSP lead to more robust policies that can adapt to different opponent strategies, and that a low-level control commands are crucial for learning performing strategies in the pursuit-evasion task. Finally, a qualitative analysis of the learned behaviours highlights the emergence of cooperative tactics among the pursuers.

</details>


### [269] [Responsible Personalisation: The Double-Edged Sword of Personalisation in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06344)
*Antonio Andriella,Jauwairia Nasir,Andrea Rezzani,Alyssa Kubota,Dimitri Lacroix,Tamlin Love,Aniol Civit,Vicky Charisi,Elisabeth Andre,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对人机交互中个性化技术带来的伦理风险碎片化问题，提出了一个基于生命周期和情境感知的个性化人机交互框架，系统分析了自主性侵蚀、偏见用户建模、操纵、去人性化和隐私侵犯等伦理风险在不同交互情境中的表现，并提供了可操作的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于负责任个性化的文献零散分布，缺乏对伦理风险在不同交互情境中如何产生的结构化理解；而机器人的具身性和社会存在感会放大、重塑甚至产生新型风险。

Method: 提出了基于生命周期和情境感知的个性化HRI框架，结合个性化过程的阶段（生命周期）与交互特征（短期vs长期、开放域vs封闭域），从具身感知视角进行系统性伦理风险分析。

Result: 框架实现了对自主性侵蚀、偏见用户建模、操纵、去人性化和隐私侵犯等关键伦理风险的系统性分析，并阐明了这些风险在不同交互情境中的表现差异。

Conclusion: 该工作为个性化机器人行为提供了更系统化、透明化和伦理基础的设计空间与风险格局，为未来个性化HRI的可信发展奠定了结构化基础。

Abstract: While personalisation is becoming a defining capability in human-robot interaction (HRI), the existing literature on responsible personalisation remains fragmented, offering isolated accounts of ethical risks without a structured understanding of how they emerge across interaction contexts. This gap is particularly critical in HRI, where robots' embodiment and social presence can amplify and reshape such risks or generate new types of risks. We present a lifecycle-based and context-sensitive framework for personalised HRI, grounded in an embodiment-aware perspective. The framework combines stages of the personalisation process with interaction characteristics (short-term vs. long-term, open-domain vs. closed-domain), enabling systematic analysis of how risks arise and evolve. Building on this, we conduct an integrative analysis of key ethical risks, including autonomy erosion, biased user modelling, manipulation, dehumanisation, and privacy violations, and examine how they manifest across contexts. We translate these insights into actionable design recommendations and outline open research challenges. By structuring both the design space and risk landscape of personalised HRI, this work provides a foundation for more systematic, transparent, and ethically grounded approaches to personalised robot behaviour.

</details>


### [270] [Delay-Aware Active Triangulation with Uncertainty-Driven Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Counter-UAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05957)
*Seungwook Lee,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种延迟感知、不确定性驱动的多智能体强化学习框架，通过AoI增强观测、感知一致性奖励和多源协方差传播，在Counter-UAS目标定位中实现0.547±0.217 m RMSE和78.1%三角测量有效性，验证了循环记忆对延迟补偿的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动视觉三角测量方法假设瞬时状态反馈，忽略了检测、通信和决策传播带来的累积延迟，导致实际部署中定位性能显著下降。

Method: (1) 基于Dec-POMDP框架，引入信息年龄(AoI)增强观测以实现延迟感知协调；(2) 提出感知一致性奖励函数替代特权清洁状态奖励，形成稳定性-鲁棒性权衡；(3) 建立多源解析协方差传播模型，融合像素、位姿、云台和内参等不确定性源。

Result: AoI提升三角测量有效性10.6个百分点；感知一致性奖励比特权奖励RMSE低0.086 m(0.547 vs 0.633 m)，跟踪丢失减少27%；仅考虑角度噪声导致RMSE恶化2.8倍；MAPPO策略达到0.547±0.217 m RMSE和78.1%三角测量有效性，而MLP策略有效性仅0.7%。

Conclusion: 延迟感知机制和循环记忆架构是实现鲁棒多智能体主动视觉定位的关键组件，AoI作为观测增强有效提升了延迟环境下的协调性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent active visual triangulation enables precise 3D localization of aerial targets by coordinating mobile observers with controllable cameras. However, existing methods assume instantaneous state feedback, ignoring cumulative latency from detection, communication, and decision propagation. We present a delay-aware, uncertainty-driven multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for target localization in Counter-UAS applications. Our contributions are: (1) a Dec-POMDP formulation with Age-of-Information (AoI) augmented observations enabling delay-aware coordination -- AoI improves triangulation validity by 10.6 percentage points; (2) a controlled comparison showing that perception-consistent rewards outperform privileged clean-state rewards (0.547 m vs.0.633 m RMSE, 27% fewer track losses) -- both policies are trained through identical observation noise but differ in what they are optimized for, producing a stability-robustness tradeoff; and (3) multi-source analytical covariance propagation incorporating pixel, pose, gimbal, and intrinsics uncertainties -- restricting to angular noise alone causes 2.8-fold RMSE degradation. Experiments with MAPPO in 4096 parallel environments achieve 0.547 +- 0.217 m RMSE with 78.1% triangulation validity, while MLP policies achieve near-zero validity (0.7%), confirming recurrent memory as essential for delay compensation.

</details>


### [271] [Imagined Rollouts are Kinematic, Not Dynamic: A Diagnosis of Long-Horizon World-Model Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05966)
*Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Korbinian Moller,Yuan Gao,Christian Oefinger,Sebastian Schmidt,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用运动学-动力学重构框架解释世界模型的长程失败：世界模型倾向于生成运动学合理的想象而非真正的动力学推演。通过定义运动学一致性误差（iKCE）作为逐帧诊断指标，并在DreamerV3/DMC walker-walk上验证发现：模型的iKCE比真实物理 rollout 高出两个数量级，且在摩擦系数跨过步态崩溃边界时保持统计平坦，而策略奖励已崩溃，呈现"运动学而非动力学"的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将世界模型的长程失败归因于"复合误差"，但这种泛化表述无法区分误差的具体类型。作者希望更精细地理解世界模型到底在哪类物理推断上出错，从而为改进提供方向。

Method: 1) 提出iKCE指标：度量 rollout 偏离闭式运动学零假设的程度；2) 设计扰动协议：测试当物理条件（如摩擦系数）跨越动力学边界时iKCE是否响应；3) 在DreamerV3/DMC walker-walk上进行摩擦系数扫描实验，对比模型iKCE与真实物理rollout的差异。

Result: 1) DreamerV3的iKCE比真实物理rollout高出约两个数量级；2) 在摩擦系数从正常到步态崩溃的扫描范围内，模型的iKCE保持统计平坦，而训练策略的奖励已显著下降；3) 诊断信号在超过步态周期的长程预测中才能区分运动学与动力学想象。

Conclusion: 世界模型的长程失败本质上是"运动学而非动力学"的问题：模型能维持运动学一致的表象，但无法捕捉动力学边界条件的变化，这揭示了复合误差中主要是动力学推断的缺失，为改进世界模型的物理建模提供了新视角。

Abstract: Long-horizon failure in world models is conventionally attributed to compounding error, a generic framing that does not distinguish what kind of error compounds. We propose a kinematic-vs-dynamic reframing: world models tend to imagine kinematically rather than dynamically. We operationalize this as the imagined Kinematic-Consistency Error, a per-step diagnostic that measures how far a rollout departs from a closed-form kinematic null, paired with a perturbation protocol that tests whether iKCE responds when physical conditions cross a regime boundary. We instantiate the diagnostic on a released DreamerV3 checkpoint trained on DMC walker-walk, where imagined iKCE runs roughly two orders of magnitude above that of matched real-physics rollouts. Across a friction sweep that crosses the gait-collapse boundary, the model's iKCE stays statistically flat even as the trained policy's reward collapses through the same range, providing the kinematic-not-dynamic signature. The diagnostic distinguishes kinematic from dynamic imagination at horizons longer than the embodiment's gait period.

</details>


### [272] [RoboTALES: Learning Reasoning-Guided Robot Policies via Task-Aligned Simulated Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06018)
*Hanan Gani,Tejal Kulkarni,Madhoolika Chodavarapu,Nicklas Hansen,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTALES提出了一种单阶段框架，利用层次化LLM规划器和VLM批评器将预训练视频生成模型与任务对齐，生成与任务意图一致的模拟未来帧，并用于训练机器人策略，在长时域任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型在视觉运动控制中具有潜力，但其想象的未来帧往往偏离任务意图，且缺乏可靠的动作条件性，难以用于规划或策略提取。

Method: 提出RoboTALES单阶段框架，包含两个创新：(1) 层次化LLM规划器，将复杂任务分解为子目标序列，引导模型想象；(2) VLM批评器，评估想象未来帧并基于奖励反馈使模型内部表征聚焦于目标。

Result: 在RoboCasa和LIBERO10的多种操作任务上评估，RoboTALES consistently优于现有方法，尤其在长时域任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过将视频生成器锚定在抽象推理上，能够产生时间一致的动作展开和更连贯的动作，有效提升了视频生成模型在机器人控制中的实用性。

Abstract: Pretrained video generative models are promising backbones for visuomotor control, but their imagined futures often drift from task intent and are not reliably action-conditional. As a result, these models can be difficult to use for planning or policy extraction. To address these limitations, we propose RoboTALES, a single-stage framework that learns task-aligned simulated futures and uses them to train robot policies. Our approach introduces two key innovations: (1) a hierarchical LLM-based planner that breaks complex tasks into a sequence of subgoals to guide the model's imagination; and (2) a VLM-based critic that evaluates these ``imagined'' futures and uses reward-based feedback to keep the model's internal representations focused on the goal. By anchoring the video generator in abstract reasoning, we produce temporally consistent rollouts and more coherent actions. We evaluate RoboTALES on diverse manipulation tasks from RoboCasa and LIBERO10, and show that our method consistently outperforms existing methods, especially in long-horizon tasks. Our code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/hananshafi/RoboTALES.

</details>


### [273] [ThorArena: Benchmarking Humanoid Physical Interaction with Human Motion-Force Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06052)
*Chenhao Yu,Hongwu Wang,Weitao Zhang,Youhao Hu,Jiachen Zhang,Gangyang Li,Alois Knoll,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ThorArena是一个基于人体示教同步运动与力测量数据的力感知人形机器人交互评测基准，通过力感知跟踪评分（FATS）等指标全面评估外力影响下的全身控制性能，实验证明力感知评测能揭示传统无外力评估中隐藏的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人数据集和基准主要关注运动学层面的动作，忽略了同步的交互力测量，导致当前评估无法捕捉外力对跟踪精度、稳定性和控制鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 1) 采集真实世界交互数据集，同步记录全身人体运动及双手施加的力；2) 提出力感知评估指标（FATS及补充诊断指标），综合评估全身跟踪精度、不同力水平下的鲁棒性、控制力和任务存活率；3) 建立统一基准协议，在仿真中回放录制的交互力并提供标准化评估接口。

Result: 对代表性全身控制策略的实验表明，力感知评估揭示了在传统无外力评估下基本隐藏的显著性能差异。

Conclusion: ThorArena为研究力感知人形机器人交互提供了实用且可复现的框架，并为评估接触丰富的人形机器人行为提供了新的基准。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly expected to perform contact-rich tasks that require not only accurate whole-body motion but also robust physical interaction with surrounding objects and humans. Although recent advances in humanoid motion imitation and whole-body control have achieved remarkable tracking performance, existing datasets and benchmarks primarily focus on kinematic motion while largely overlooking synchronized interaction forces. As a result, current evaluations fail to capture how external interaction forces affect tracking accuracy, stability, and control robustness.
  In this paper, we present ThorArena, a benchmark for evaluating force-aware humanoid interaction based on human demonstrations with synchronized motion and force measurements. We collect a real-world interaction dataset that simultaneously captures whole-body human motion and forces exerted by both hands across six representative physical interaction tasks. Based on these demonstrations, we propose force-aware evaluation metrics that jointly assess whole-body tracking accuracy, robustness under different force levels, control effort, and episode survival through the Force-Aware Tracking Score (FATS) and complementary diagnostic metrics. We further establish a unified benchmark protocol that replays recorded interaction forces in simulation and provides a standardized evaluation interface for different humanoid control policies.
  Experiments on representative whole-body control policies demonstrate that force-aware evaluation reveals substantial performance differences that remain largely hidden under conventional no-force evaluation. ThorArena provides a practical and reproducible framework for studying force-aware humanoid interaction and offers a new benchmark for evaluating contact-rich humanoid behaviors.

</details>


### [274] [MP-MPPI: A Motion Primitive Guided Sampling-Based Optimizer for Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06123)
*Marlon Mathisen,Aksel Vaaler,Olav Egeland,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将运动基元融入模型预测路径积分（MPPI）方法的算法，通过结构化采样增强全局最优解的收敛性，并在四旋翼仿真器的障碍场导航任务中验证了其提升控制空间探索能力的同时保持实时控制所需的快速响应特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的控制器在路径规划能力上存在局限性，难以保证收敛到全局最优解，需要一种能兼顾探索能力和实时响应性的方法。

Method: 在MPPI的实时采样优化循环中，同时评估运动基元（结构化采样）和扰动控制序列，以增强对控制空间的探索。

Result: 在四旋翼仿真器的障碍场导航任务中，该方法在增强控制空间探索的同时，保持了实时控制所需的快速、响应性行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效提升了采样控制器向全局最优解的收敛能力，在不牺牲实时性的前提下改善了路径规划性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel method that extends the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) method with motion primitives for additional structured sampling, which enhances the convergence towards a globally optimal solution. By evaluating motion primitives and perturbed control sequences in a real-time sampling-based optimization loop, this work addresses the limitations of the path planning capabilities of sampling-based controllers. The algorithm is implemented on a quadcopter simulator and tested on an obstacle field navigation task. It is demonstrated that the proposed approach enhances exploration of the control space while maintaining the fast, reactive behavior required for real-time control.

</details>


### [275] [EAGOR: Embodied Reasoning in Omni-direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06165)
*Shriram Damodaran,Soumyaratna Debnath,Yan Wu,Wei-Yun Yau,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EAGOR 提出了一种无需训练、几何感知的框架，用于具身智能体的 360° 方向推理。该方法利用球谐函数表示在球面上进行递归贝叶斯方向估计，而非在等距柱状投影图像上操作，从而避免了投影畸变和视角变换下的方向不一致问题。在 HOS 和 OSR-Bench 基准上分别取得 +34.4% 和 +45.6% 的相对增益，导航成功率提升 14.6%，步数减少 17.7%，平均角度误差降低 24.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 将 360° 观测投影为 2D ERP 图像后用透视图像架构处理，忽略了 360° 观测的球面本质（每个像素代表相对智能体的视角方向），导致在智能体运动引起的相机视角变换下方向估计不一致，尤其影响无地图导航任务。

Method: 提出 EAGOR 框架：1) 将方向推理建模为球面上的递归贝叶斯估计，而非预测 ERP 图像坐标；2) 引入球谐函数信念场（SH-BF），利用球谐表示提供全局定义的旋转感知基，在智能体运动下等变地传播信念；3) 无需训练骨干 VLM。

Result: 在 HOS 和 OSR-Bench 上分别取得 +34.4% 和 +45.6% 的平均相对增益；导航成功率提升 +14.6%，步数减少 17.7%，平均角度误差降低 24.5%。

Conclusion: EAGOR 通过球面几何感知的无需训练框架，有效解决了 360° 方向推理中的视角变换不一致问题，在多个基准和真实机器人实验中均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Omni-directional (360°) cameras provide embodied agents with a holistic view of their surroundings, making them suited for directional reasoning in tasks such as navigation and object search. Existing Vision Language Models (VLMs) project 360° observations to 2D equirectangular projection (ERP) images and process them using architectures designed for perspective images. However, they ignore the spherical nature of 360° observations, where each pixel represents a viewing direction relative to the agent. Consequently, their direction estimates often become inconsistent under camera view transformations caused by agent motion. This limitation is particularly critical for map-free navigation, where the agent must continuously estimate the target direction in its egocentric frame. We propose EAGOR, a training-free, geometry-aware framework for embodied 360° directional reasoning. Instead of predicting target directions as ERP image coordinates, EAGOR formulates directional reasoning as recursive Bayesian estimation directly on the sphere. It maintains a continuous belief over target directions and propagates it equivariantly under agent motion without training the backbone VLMs. To achieve this, we introduce the Spherical Harmonic Belief Field (SH-BF), whose spherical harmonic representation provides a globally defined, rotation-aware basis for directional estimation on the spherical manifold. This formulation eliminates ERP seam discontinuities, latitude distortions, and interpolation errors. We evaluate EAGOR on two benchmark datasets and real-world experiments with a legged robot across directional reasoning tasks. EAGOR consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving average relative gains of +34.4% and +45.6% on HOS and OSR-Bench, respectively, while improving navigation success by +14.6%, reducing step count by 17.7%, and lowering mean angular error by 24.5%.

</details>


### [276] [Calf-Integrated Arms for Bimanual Quadruped Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06186)
*Yan Pan,Yuanchuan Ren,Chipui Chan,Jingcheng Sun,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将带有棱柱滑块、两个旋转关节和夹爪的机械臂集成到Unitree Go2机器狗的前小腿上，实现四足站立下的双臂操作，并通过视觉语言模型进行长时程自主技能调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人操作设计在操作能力与站立稳定性之间存在权衡：躯干安装的机械臂位置高且通常为单臂；用腿作为操作臂会导致操作腿离地；腿装夹爪需要后腿站立才能完成双手任务。本文旨在设计一种能够在四足站立状态下完成双手操作任务的新方案。

Method: 在每个前小腿上集成一个包含棱柱滑块、两个旋转关节和夹爪的机械臂；利用视觉语言模型在预定义技能库的边界处进行技能选择，以头戴相机图像和任务状态为条件，实现长时程自主任务调度。

Result: 在仿真中，该设计成功完成了三项双手操作任务：长时程柜子任务（自主技能选择）、协作双手抬升、双臂间交接。

Conclusion: 将操作臂集成到四足机器人前小腿上，可在保持四足站立的同时实现双手操作能力，结合视觉语言模型实现长时程自主任务执行。

Abstract: Most quadruped loco-manipulation designs trade manipulation capability against stance. A trunk-mounted arm sits high and usually carries a single arm; using the legs as manipulators lifts the manipulating leg off the ground; and even leg-mounted grippers reach two-handed tasks only by rearing onto the hind legs. This paper integrates a manipulator with a prismatic slider, two revolute joints, and a gripper into each front calf of a Unitree Go2. The two arms grasp objects at ground level and manipulate with both hands while all four feet stay planted, without rearing. With one arm carrying, the base stays free to walk. A vision-language model sequences skills from a predefined library at each skill boundary, conditioned on the head-camera image and task state, for long-horizon autonomy. In simulation, the design performs three bimanual tasks: a long-horizon cabinet task under autonomous skill selection, a cooperative two-handed lift, and an inter-arm handover.

</details>


### [277] [APVI-SLAM: Real-Time Acoustic-Pressure-Visual-Inertial Localization and Photorealistic Mapping System in Complex Underwater Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06222)
*Hanwen Zhang,Yipeng Zhu,Xiaopeng Guo,Huajian Huang,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APVI-SLAM是一个面向极端水下环境的实时多传感器融合SLAM系统，通过可靠性感知定位框架、滑动窗口冻结恢复策略和四叉树引导的高效建图模块，实现了高精度定位与逼真场景重建，并在公开和自建数据集上达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端水下环境导致视觉惯性SLAM中特征退化严重和估计器发散，而现有方法在间歇性视觉失效场景下的鲁棒多传感器融合仍存在挑战，且缺乏水下建图评估基准。

Method: 提出了可靠性感知定位框架（动态重加权传感器估计器）和滑动窗口冻结策略（从跟踪失效中恢复），同时设计了高效四叉树引导建图模块（增量式水介质建模与3D高斯优化）。

Result: 在公开和自建珊瑚礁调查数据集上，APVI-SLAM在实时性下实现了最先进的定位精度和场景重建质量。

Conclusion: APVI-SLAM通过创新的多传感器融合与建图策略，有效解决了极端水下环境下的鲁棒定位与高质量建图问题，同时提供的多模态基准数据集有助于推动水下建图评估研究。

Abstract: Extreme subsea environments often cause severe feature de-gradation and estimator divergence in underwater visual-inertial SLAM. Although sensors like Doppler Velocity Logs (DVL) and pressure gauges provide auxiliary constraints, robust multi-sensor fusion during intermittent visual failure remains challenging. To address this, we present APVI-SLAM, a real-time multi-sensor fusion SLAM system that achieves both accurate underwater localization and photorealistic mapping. Our approach introduces a reliability-aware localization framework that dynamically reweights sensor estimators and employs a sliding-window freezing strategy to recover from tracking failures, substantially enhancing system robustness. Furthermore, for high-fidelity scenes reconstruction, we propose an efficient quadtree-guided mapping module that facilitates incremental water-medium modeling and 3D Gaussian optimization. Recognizing the lack of benchmark for underwater mapping evaluation, we also contribute a coral reef surveying dataset with synchronized multi-modality data. Extensive experiments on public and our proposed benchmarks demonstrate that APVI-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art localization and reconstruction quality at real-time speeds.

</details>


### [278] [RoboVAST: Automated Scenario-Based Validation of Robots at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06248)
*Frederik Pasch,Samuel Wiest,Argentina Ortega,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于场景的机器人系统验证方法学，通过组合式场景建模、声明式测试规约和容器化并行执行，实现了大规模自动化验证。基于此开发的RoboVAST框架在5个室内地图上生成了5480个场景配置、超过10万次执行（总计1800+小时模拟、1873公里行驶），并通过每个配置20次重复运行有效区分系统故障与随机异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统验证严重依赖人工经验进行场景选择与变体设计，难以规模化，损害了可复现性和验证结论的可靠性。

Method: 提出组合式场景建模方法，形式化定义场景维度的变化、实例化、执行与解释；开发RoboVAST框架，包含声明式测试规约、插件化场景生成和可扩展容器化执行与结果分析。

Result: 在5个室内地图上生成了5480个场景配置、超过10万次运行，总计1800+小时模拟和1873公里行驶；每个配置重复20次以区分系统故障与随机异常。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够自动化、可复现地执行大规模机器人系统验证，并通过统计重复有效区分确定性故障与随机性异常，增强了验证结论的可信度。

Abstract: Validation of robotic systems critically depends on the operating conditions under which they are assessed. Scenario selection and variation are often manual, experience-driven, and difficult to scale, which harms reproducibility and weakens validation conclusions. We propose a scenario-based methodology that models scenarios compositionally and formalizes how these dimensions are varied, instantiated, executed, and interpreted. Building on this, we introduce RoboVAST, a framework that realizes declarative campaign specifications, plugin-based scenario generation, and scalable containerized execution with integrated result analysis. We demonstrate the approach with a navigation dataset comprising 5480 scenario configurations and over 100000 execution runs across five indoor maps with varied paths, sensor noise, software parameters, and obstacle settings, totaling more than 1800 hours of simulated operation and 1873 km traveled. Twenty repetitions per configuration allow us to distinguish systematic failures from stochastic anomalies.

</details>


### [279] [Diagnosing Semantic Handoff Failures in Agent-Orchestrated Vision-Language-Action Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06256)
*Ke Rui,Yushen Zuo,Jiawei Wang,Haoran Jia,Jinming Ma,Weitao Zhou,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究BEHAVIOR-1K中长时序家庭任务的语义交接问题，构建了智能体编排的视觉-语言-动作执行框架。通过多视角视觉-语言模型验证，对比干净初始状态与链式状态下的技能执行成功率，发现单个技能在干净状态下表现良好（77-100%），但链式状态下频繁失败，揭示了单技能成功与长时序任务完成之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序家庭任务中，机器人需组合多个语言条件技能，但技能间的语义边界不明确，前序技能的终止状态导致后续技能无法可靠启动，现有研究缺乏对这一"语义交接"问题的系统诊断方法。

Method: 构建智能体编排的VLA执行框架：使用基于π₀.5的技能检查点、为每个技能分配类型参数和步数预算、采用多视角视觉-语言模型验证来决定执行是否推进、重试或重规划。通过在干净技能边界快照和链式终态两种初始状态分布下评估同一检查点来分离单技能能力与长时序组合鲁棒性。

Result: 导航、抓取、放置和开门等技能在干净快照下成功率为77-100%，但链式状态下的组合 rollout 频繁失败。失败可归因于下一技能就绪性、目标定位和底层控制执行三类问题。

Conclusion: 单技能成功与可靠的长时序任务完成之间存在显著差距，未来VLA技能库必须学习对链式状态分布的鲁棒性，而干净演示系统性地低估了这种分布的重要性。

Abstract: Long-horizon household tasks require robots to compose many language-conditioned skills, yet the boundary between consecutive skills is rarely explicit. A skill may satisfy its own postcondition while leaving the robot, objects, or camera views in a state from which the next skill cannot reliably start. We study this semantic handoff problem in BEHAVIOR-1K through an agent-orchestrated vision-language-action execution harness. The harness invokes $π_{0.5}$-based skill checkpoints trained from cleaned BEHAVIOR-1K demonstrations, assigns each skill typed arguments and a step budget, and uses multi-view vision-language model verification to decide whether execution should advance, retry, or replan. To separate isolated skill competence from long-horizon compositional robustness, we evaluate the same checkpoints under two initial-state distributions: clean skill-boundary snapshots and chained terminal states produced by previous skills. Selected navigation, grasping, placement, and door-opening skills achieve 77--100% success from clean snapshots under human-reviewed verification, yet composed rollouts still frequently stall from chained states. Execution traces attribute these failures to next-skill readiness, target grounding, and low-level control execution, revealing a substantial gap between single-skill success and reliable long-horizon task completion. These findings turn near-zero end-to-end task success into actionable diagnostics, showing that future VLA skill libraries must learn robustness to the messy chained-state distribution that clean demonstrations systematically underrepresent.

</details>


### [280] [Optimal Transport Q-Learning for Flow Policy Steering and Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06262)
*Andreas Sochopoulos,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Nikolaos Tsagkas,João Moura,Michael Gienger,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出最优传输Q学习(OTQL)，通过强化学习后训练，利用机器人自身经验微调并加速次优的流策略，在50-60轮交互预算内将单任务策略成功率从36%提升至86%，VLA模型从38%提升至76%，同时减少70%的推理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流策略在机器人VLA模型中表现优异，但高性能需要快速推理和高质量演示数据，而这些数据难以获取；缺乏这些条件会导致策略次优且在分布偏移下失败。

Method: 提出OTQL方法，利用优势加权条件最优传输流匹配(advantage weighted conditional optimal transport flow matching)来微调流策略，通过RL后训练使用机器人自身经验进行优化。

Result: 单任务策略成功率从36%提升至86%，预训练VLA模型从38%提升至76%，每个动作生成的推理步骤减少70%，且仅在50-60轮交互预算内完成。

Conclusion: OTQL能够有效后训练流策略，利用机器人自身经验显著提升策略性能并加速推理，无需计算昂贵的蒸馏过程，适用于仿真和真实机器人任务。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies have recently demonstrated remarkable performance in robotic applications by accurately capturing multimodal robot trajectory distributions, especially in the context of vision language action (VLA) models. However, high quality policy performance also requires fast inference and high quality demonstrations, which are often hard to get. Lack of these leads to suboptimal policy behaviors and failure under distribution shifts. In this work we address the problem of fine-tuning and accelerating suboptimal flow-based policies using the robot's experience through RL post-training. We introduce Optimal Transport Q-Learning (OTQL), a new method for finetuning flow policies using advantage weighted conditional optimal transport flow matching. OTQL can finetune and accelerate flows with an interaction budget of 50-60 episodes while avoiding computationally expensive distillation in simulation and real-world robot tasks. Our results show that OTQL post-trains flow policies using the robot's own experience, increasing average success percentage of single-task policies from 36% to 86% and of a pre-trained VLA from 38% to 76% while reducing the number of inference steps per action generation by 70%.

</details>


### [281] [LAMP: Latent Motion Prior-Guided Real-World Learning for Dexterous Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06323)
*Xinye Yang,Zhiyuan Ma,Hongze Yu,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang,Xiaojie Chai,Xinlei Chen,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜在运动先验的灵巧手操作学习框架，通过将高维手部动作压缩到历史条件潜在空间，结合模仿学习和在线强化学习，在仅需少量示范的情况下实现了高效、安全的真实世界灵巧操作，最终达到98.75%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的灵巧手学习因高维动作空间导致模仿误差放大和强化学习探索易破坏接触状态而脆弱。现有方法将模仿学习与在线强化学习结合虽减少了人工监督，但在原始高维手部动作空间中的无约束探索样本效率低且对物理硬件存在风险。

Method: 提出潜在运动先验模块，将近期手部动作历史映射为紧凑的历史条件潜在先验，并将连续潜在命令解码为可执行的高维手部目标。基于此，构建三阶段框架：从示范中预训练先验模块，训练视觉运动策略预测原始手臂命令和潜在手部动作偏移，最后在相同潜在空间中通过在线残差强化学习优化策略。

Result: 在四个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，仅需少量任务特定示范，该方法初始模仿学习成功率达56.25%，经在线强化学习后提升至98.75%，其中三个任务达到100%，一个达到95%。

Conclusion: 所提出的共享可解码潜在动作接口使残差探索能够在示范的接触一致性手部动作附近进行局部修正，而非独立扰动每个手指关节，显著提升了样本效率和安全性，实现了从少量示范到高成功率的灵巧操作学习。

Abstract: Real-world learning for dexterous hands remains brittle because high-dimensional hand actions amplify imitation errors and make reinforcement-learning exploration prone to contact-breaking motion. While combining imitation learning (IL) with online reinforcement learning (RL) can reduce manual supervision, unconstrained exploration in raw hand-action spaces is sample-inefficient and risky for physical hardware. We introduce a latent motion prior module (\prior{}) that maps recent hand-action histories to a compact, history-conditioned latent prior and decodes continuous latent commands into executable high-dimensional hand targets. Built on this prior, \method{} is a three-stage real-world dexterous learning framework: it pretrains \prior{} from demonstrations, trains a visuomotor policy that predicts native arm commands and latent hand-action offsets, and improves the policy with online residual RL in the same latent hand-action space. This shared, decodable interface lets residual exploration make local corrections near demonstrated, contact-consistent hand motions rather than perturbing every finger joint independently. We evaluate \method{} on four real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks against raw, linear, and discrete hand-action interfaces. Starting from small task-specific demonstration sets, \method{} achieves a 56.25\% average IL success rate and raises it to 98.75\% after online RL, reaching 100\% final success on three tasks and 95\% on the remaining task.

</details>


### [282] [OrchardBench: A Physically-Grounded, GPU-Parallel Apple-Orchard Simulation Benchmark for Agricultural Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06337)
*Humphrey Munn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OrchardBench是一个基于Newton引擎的GPU并行物理仿真平台，用于苹果园机器人采摘研究。它通过L-system随机生成树木，模拟树枝的弹性、断裂、果实脱离等物理特性，所有参数均有文献依据。支持多环境并行、领域随机化，并定义了收获完整性、吞吐量和植物损伤等评估指标。基准测试显示，传统方法仅能收获约八分之一的可达果实，为新型自主方法留下了空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人果树采摘研究受限于田间实验的高成本、低可重复性：果园每年仅数周可用、每棵树形态各异、控制误差会永久损伤作物或植株。现有图形学和农学中的树木模型几何精细但缺乏物理交互，而机器人学习中使用的GPU并行模拟器又不包含逼真的树木模型。

Method: 基于Newton引擎构建GPU并行物理仿真。树木由随机L-system生长并实例化为全铰接体：树枝为符合欧拉-伯努利梁理论的扭簧阻尼器，可依据木材断裂模量断裂；苹果为独立刚体，通过茎绳连接，在文献支撑的拉力下脱离；可移动、密度可控的 foliage 层模拟真实叶片遮挡效果。每个物理参数均有已发表文献作为依据。采用逐环境领域随机化使每个批次的世界为不同树木，移动机械臂配备腕部深度相机实现几何果实感知与自主采摘闭环。

Result: 基准方法在检测到的果实上成功率达约40%，仅能收获树上约八分之一的可达果实，留出了充分的改进空间。

Conclusion: OrchardBench提供了一个物理真实、可扩展的仿真平台，解决了果树采摘研究中实验可重复性和成本瓶颈问题，其定义的评估指标和留下的性能空间为新型自主采摘方法的发展提供了基础。

Abstract: Robotic tree-fruit harvesting is a flagship problem for agricultural automation, but progress is bottlenecked by the cost and irreproducibility of field experiments: an orchard is available only weeks a year, every tree is different, and a control error can permanently damage the crop or the plant. The tree models used in graphics and agronomy are geometrically detailed but physically inert, while the GPU-parallel simulators used in robot learning contain no plausible trees. We present OrchardBench, a physically-grounded, GPU-parallel simulation of apple-orchard trees on the Newton engine. Each tree is grown by a stochastic L-system and instantiated as a fully articulated body: branches are compliant torsional spring-dampers whose stiffness follows Euler-Bernoulli beam theory, they break at a wood modulus of rupture and fall as free hinges, and apples are independent bodies on stem tethers that detach at literature-grounded pull forces and load the branch when pulled. A moving, density-controllable foliage layer occludes the canopy as real leaves do. Every physical parameter is tied to a published source. Per-environment domain randomization makes each batched world a distinct tree, and a mobile manipulator with a wrist depth camera closes the loop with geometric fruit perception and an autonomous harvesting baseline. Careful engineering of the solver and the model lets OrchardBench run many parallel environments at interactive rates on a laptop GPU. We define the tasks and a metric suite spanning harvest completeness, throughput, and plant damage (with a per-canopy-zone breakdown), and report baseline results across foliage, fruit load, terrain, canopy zone, and parallelism. The analytic baseline succeeds on about 40% of the fruit it detects and harvests only about an eighth of the reachable fruit on a tree, leaving clear headroom for novel autonomy approaches.

</details>


### [283] [Training-Free Acceleration for Vision-Language-Action Models with Action Caching and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06370)
*Ryuji Oi,Hikari Otsuka,Kosuke Matsushima,Yuki Ichikawa,Masato Motomura,Tatsuya Kaneko,Daichi Fujiki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionCache 是一种即插即用的外部缓存，通过复用历史中间动作来加速流匹配 VLA 模型的推理，在 π0.5 和 GR00T-N1.6 上分别实现高达 11.75 倍和 34.43 倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配 VLA 模型的迭代去噪过程是计算瓶颈，严重限制了实时部署。

Method: 构建外部缓存 ActionCache，存储中间动作及其紧凑多模态键，通过检索相似历史上下文（跨回合甚至跨任务）来热启动动作生成，减少去噪迭代次数。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中保持高任务成功率，对 π0.5 和 GR00T-N1.6 分别实现 11.75 倍和 34.43 倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: ActionCache 有效解决了流匹配 VLA 模型的实时部署瓶颈，是一种即插即用的高效加速方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for generalizable robotic manipulations. In particular, flow matching-based VLA models have shown remarkable success due to their capability to generate precise and smooth action sequences and capture multimodal distributions. However, the iterative denoising process in the action head acts as a major computational bottleneck, posing a critical challenge for real-time deployment. To address this challenge, we propose ActionCache, a plug-and-play external cache that opportunistically reuses past intermediate actions to warm-start generations from the vicinity of target actions, thereby drastically reducing the inference latency. Specifically, ActionCache stores the intermediate actions with compact multimodal keys, which enables retrieval from similar past contexts across different episodes or even different tasks. Experimental results in simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that ActionCache maintains high task success rates in a low-latency regime, achieving inference acceleration of up to $11.75\times$ and $34.43\times$ for representative flow-based VLA models, $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.6, respectively.

</details>


### [284] [Towards Real-World Applications with an Autonomous Powered Wheelchair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06383)
*Simone Arreghini,Alessandro Giusti,Alex Bordini,Enrico Ferrara,Giovanni Fulgoni,Antonio Paolillo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将自主感知、手势交互与导航能力集成到商用自平衡电动轮椅（Genny Zero）上的概念验证原型，实现了远程召唤和人员跟随两种辅助移动应用，展示了自主机器人技术与辅助移动平台结合的可行性，同时指出了技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮椅使用者需要具备主动支持、适应动态环境且直观易用的辅助移动系统，但现有电动轮椅自主性有限，缺乏与先进感知和导航能力的有效集成，尤其在复杂真实环境中。

Method: 基于商用自平衡轮椅Genny Zero（通过重心移动实现免提操作），集成RGB-D相机（用于人感知与人机交互）和LiDAR传感器（用于定位与导航），构建自主感知、手势交互与导航系统。

Result: 在两种辅助应用场景中成功演示了系统功能：(i) 远程召唤轮椅；(ii) 人员跟随（采用领导者-跟随者策略），并包含受限室内导航示例。实验结果验证了集成方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将自主机器人技术融入辅助移动平台的潜力，同时指出在迈向用户可用、智能化移动解决方案前仍需解决诸多技术挑战。

Abstract: Wheelchair users call for assistive mobility systems that provide active support, adapt to dynamic environments, and are intuitive and user-friendly.
