However, powered wheelchairs typically still provide limited autonomy and lack effective integration with advanced perception and navigation capabilities, particularly in complex real-world environments. This paper presents a preliminary study toward autonomous powered wheelchairs for real-world assistive mobility. We introduce a proof-of-concept prototype that integrates autonomous perception, gesture-based interaction, and navigation on a commercially available self-balancing powered wheelchair. The proposed system builds upon Genny Zero, a commercial self-balancing wheelchair that enables hands-free and intuitive operation through body-weight shifting. To extend its capabilities toward autonomous operation, we integrate an RGB-D camera for human-aware perception and interaction, together with a LiDAR sensor for localization and navigation. We demonstrate the integrated system in two assistive applications: (i) hailing, allowing users to call the wheelchair from a distance; and (ii) people-following, where the wheelchair follows a person using leader-follower strategies, including a constrained indoor navigation example. The results highlight the potential of combining autonomous robotics with assistive mobility platforms, while also showing the feasibility of the proposed integration and identifying the main technical challenges that must be addressed before moving toward user-ready, accessible, and intelligent mobility solutions. A video demonstrating the experimental setup and results is available at: https://youtu.be/LVAix_Qx7bM.

</details>


### [285] [Learning to Throw Objects Safely in Multi-Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06388)
*Mohammadreza Kasaei,Klemen Voncina,Hamidreza Kasaei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种势场状态表示（PFR）方法，通过紧凑编码篮筐吸引力和障碍物斥力，使强化学习策略能够在杂乱环境中实现鲁棒的机器人投掷，在真实机器人实验中达到90%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人投掷方法（如TossingBot）假设无障碍物环境，无法应对现实中的杂乱场景。本文旨在解决在有随机放置障碍物的环境中，将物体可靠投掷到目标篮筐的问题。

Method: 提出势场状态表示（PFR），在固定大小网格上紧凑编码篮筐吸引力和障碍物斥力，使RL策略能泛化到任意数量和配置的障碍物。策略通过动觉示教初始化，使用三种SOTA算法（SAC、DDPG、TD3）在仿真中优化。

Result: SAC在多种场景下表现最一致；PFR相比显式状态编码获得更高成功率和更好的泛化能力；真实机器人实验在杂乱场景中达到90%成功率，验证了稳健的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: PFR为无结构环境中安全高效的机器人投掷提供了一种实用且鲁棒的表示方法。

Abstract: Robotic throwing enables fast and efficient object placement beyond the robot's immediate workspace, but reliable throwing in cluttered environments remains underexplored. Existing approaches, such as TossingBot, learn throwing strategies from visual input but assume obstacle-free settings. In this paper, we address the problem of throwing objects into a target basket while avoiding obstacles placed randomly in the scene. We introduce a potential field state representation that compactly encodes both basket attraction and obstacle repulsion on a fixed-size grid, enabling reinforcement learning (RL) policies to generalize across arbitrary numbers and configurations of obstacles. The policy is initialized from kinesthetic demonstrations and optimized in simulation using three state-of-the-art RL algorithms (SAC, DDPG, TD3). Among these, SAC achieves the most consistent performance across scenarios. We compare the potential field representation against explicit state encodings and demonstrate that it achieves higher success rates and better scalability to unseen obstacle configurations. Real-robot experiments with unseen throwable objects confirm robust sim-to-real transfer, achieving up to $90\%$ success in cluttered scenes. These results demonstrate that PFR provides a practical and robust representation for safe and efficient robotic throwing in unstructured environments. A video showcasing our experiments is available at: https://youtu.be/ZZnJf8ua2dE

</details>


### [286] [From Foundation to Application: Improving VLA Models in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06403)
*Wei Wu,Fangjing Wang,Fan Lu,He Sun,Shi Liu,Yunnan Wang,Yibin Yan,Yong Wang,Shuailei Ma,Xinyang Wang,Yibin Liu,Shuai Yang,Tianxiang Zhou,Kejia Zhang,Lei Zhou,Cheng Su,Nan Xue,Bin Tan,Han Zhang,Youchao Zhang,Fei Liao,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Kecheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LingBot-VLA 2.0 通过改进数据处理、扩展动作空间和引入预测动力学建模，提升了VLA基础模型在跨任务、跨实体和长时域操作方面的泛化能力，在GM-100基准测试中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室条件与真实世界应用之间的差距阻碍了VLA基础模型的实际部署，需要弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: (1) 重构数据流水线，收集约6万小时预训练数据（5万小时机器人轨迹+1万小时第一人称人类视频）；(2) 扩展动作空间至头部、腰部、移动基座和灵巧手等自由度；(3) 引入预测动力学建模，利用视频表征模型提供语义先验、深度估计模型提供几何线索，将未来预测作为代理任务。

Result: 在GM-100基准测试中验证了改进的有效性，并在两种机器人平台上展现出跨实体的长时域移动操作能力。

Conclusion: LingBot-VLA 2.0通过数据规模化、动作空间扩展和时序建模三方面改进，有效缩小了VLA模型在实验室与真实场景间的性能差距。

Abstract: Despite recent progress of VLA foundation models, the disparity between laboratory conditions and real-world applications continues to impede their practical implementation. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VLA 2.0, which advances LingBot-VLA through improvements in three functional domains. (1) Generalization across tasks and embodiments. Compared to the previous version, we revamp the data processing pipeline and curate around 60,000 hours of data for pretraining, including 50,000 hours of robot trajectories spanning 20 robot configurations and 10,000 hours of egocentric human videos. (2) Expanded action space in addition to dual-arm hardware platforms. In particular, our system accommodates degrees of freedom for the heads, waists, mobile bases, and dexterous hands, thereby empowering the robots to tackle more complex tasks in practical scenarios. (3) Predictive dynamics modeling for improved temporal reasoning. Specifically, we formulate future prediction as a proxy task, facilitated by a video representation model for semantic priors and a depth estimation model for geometric cues. Evaluations on the GM-100 benchmark, conducted in a generalist setting, validate the beneficial impact of these proposed modifications. Furthermore, benefiting from the expanded pretraining data that covers whole-body degrees of freedom, LingBot-VLA-2.0 demonstrates strong cross-embodiment long-horizon mobile manipulation capability across the two robotic platforms.

</details>


### [287] [WristMimic: Full-Body Humanoid Control with Wrist-Guided Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06438)
*Wongyun Yu,Youngwoon Kim,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WristMimic提出了一种以手腕引导的全身控制框架，将无接触的身体运动与有接触的手指操作分离，通过手腕的 kinematic 跟踪和物体交互结果来学习抓取行为，无需直接监督手指姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法直接跟踪手部位置轨迹无法指定操作所需的接触力，且过度约束手指行为，导致物理仿真中的人-物交互表现不佳。

Method: 以手腕作为自然分界：身体和手腕通过运动学姿态目标引导（无接触区域），手指则不受人手姿态直接监督，而是通过物体跟踪和接触结果学习抓取与操作行为，并引入手腕特定的重置约束和奖励优先级。

Result: 在性能上与使用全手指姿态监督的方法相当或更优，同时实现了跨不同手部形态的手指无关重定向。

Conclusion: 手腕是分离无接触身体运动与有接触手指操作的关键枢纽，该方法在减少监督信号的同时提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: Retargeting human object interaction demonstrations to physics based simulation requires reproducing not only body motion but also the object motion and contacts that make manipulation succeed. However, position only hand trajectories do not specify the contact forces needed to manipulate objects, and directly tracking them can overconstrain contact rich finger behavior. We introduce WristMimic, a wrist guided whole body control framework that explicitly separates contact free body motion from contact rich hand manipulation. The contact free body and wrist are guided by kinematic pose targets, whereas the fingers are not directly supervised by human hand pose. Instead, they learn grasping and manipulation behaviors from object tracking and contact outcomes. Our key insight is that the wrist is the natural gate between these two regimes. It is largely free from contact and can be tracked kinematically, yet it determines the global hand configuration and places the fingers within reachable grasp affordances. To ensure reliable wrist placement during interaction, we introduce wrist specific reset constraints and reward prioritization. Experiments show that WristMimic matches or surpasses methods using full finger pose supervision while enabling finger agnostic retargeting across diverse hand embodiments.

</details>


### [288] [SIEVE: Structure-Aware Data Selection for Imitation Learning with VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06442)
*Changti Wu,Bin Yu,Zhaolong Shen,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Lei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIEVE方法，通过发现可复用的视觉运动基元和转换接口，对演示数据进行结构感知的选择，在仅使用50%数据和50%训练步数的情况下超越全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖大规模模仿学习，但数据中存在冗余、噪声和覆盖不均问题，而现有数据选择方法仅在轨迹或状态-动作层面评估，忽略了构成长程行为的可复用结构。

Method: SIEVE首先从分割轨迹中发现视觉运动基元，然后通过最大化结构暴露程度（考虑边际收益递减）将选择预算分配给不同的组合模式，最后在每个组合模式桶中选择中心轨迹以保留稳定且利于模仿的演示。

Result: 在多个数据集、基准和VLA模型上，SIEVE一致优于竞争性的数据选择基线；仅用50%演示和50%训练步骤即可超越全量数据训练。

Conclusion: 通过基元和转换捕获的可复用结构是高效VLA模仿学习的重要信号。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically trained by imitation learning on large-scale robot demonstration datasets, but more data does not necessarily yield better policies due to redundancy, noise, and uneven coverage. Existing data selection methods often assess demonstrations at either the trajectory or state-action level, missing the reusable structures that compose long-horizon behaviors. In this paper, we propose SIEVE, a structure-aware data selection method for VLA imitation learning. SIEVE views demonstrations as compositions of reusable primitives and transition interfaces. It first discovers visuo-motor primitives from segmented trajectories, then allocates selection budgets to composition patterns by maximizing reuse-aware structural exposure under diminishing returns. Finally, it selects medoid trajectories within each composition-pattern bucket to retain central, stable, and imitation-friendly demonstrations. Experiments across multiple datasets, benchmarks, and VLA models show that SIEVE consistently outperforms competitive data selection baselines. Notably, SIEVE can surpass full-data training while using only 50% of demonstrations and 50% of training steps, suggesting that reusable structure, captured through primitives and transitions, is an important signal for efficient VLA imitation learning.

</details>


### [289] [Hilti-Trimble-Oxford Dataset: 360 Visual-Inertial Benchmark with Floor Plan Priors for SLAM and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06464)
*Samuele Centanni,Yuhao Zhang,Yifu Tao,Julien Kindle,Frank Neuhaus,Tilman Koß,Aryaman Patel,Michael Helmberger,Emilia Szymańska,Torben Gräber,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个在活跃建筑工地采集的高质量视觉-惯性数据集，包含7层楼、8个月期间的30个序列，用于支持建筑工地的自动化进度监测。同时报告了一项公开研究挑战的结果，其中SLAM（62支队伍）比地板平面定位（22支队伍）吸引了更多参与者，且定位误差更高，表明该任务仍需进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光雷达建图成本高昂，而消费级360摄像头结合IMU提供了成本更低的替代方案，但需要高精度的视觉SLAM和楼层平面定位系统来支持建筑工地的变化检测与进度监测。现有数据集缺乏真实建筑工地的复杂挑战（如光照变化、移动工人、快速运动、重复结构），因此需要构建相应数据集推动研究。

Method: 在活跃建筑工地的7层楼、8个月内采集了30个视觉-惯性序列，使用高精度激光雷达-惯性SLAM系统（刚性固定在360摄像头上）获取地面真值轨迹。组织公开挑战赛评估全球最佳的视觉SLAM和定位系统。

Result: SLAM挑战吸引了62支队伍，楼层平面定位仅有22支队伍；定位任务的误差更高，凸显了建筑工地定位任务的难度。

Conclusion: 建筑工地的视觉SLAM和定位仍具挑战性，尤其是楼层平面参考定位，该数据集旨在支持这一方向的持续研究。

Abstract: Automated progress monitoring on construction sites is an active area of research and development. Robot and human-carried mapping systems have been developed to build 3D maps of building and infrastructure projects. While LiDAR-based mapping systems achieve high accuracy, the cost of LiDAR can be prohibitive. Consumer-grade cameras with wide field of view ("360 cameras") combined with embedded inertial measurement units (IMUs) provide a cost-effective alternative. To support change detection and progress monitoring, highly accurate visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) and floor plan-referenced localization systems are required. In this paper we present a high-quality dataset collected at an active construction site, which captures realistic challenges such as variable lighting conditions, moving workers, fast motions, and repetitive structures. The dataset offers thirty visual-inertial sequences recorded across seven floors over an eight-month period of the construction project. Ground truth trajectories were collected using a high quality LiDAR-inertial SLAM system rigidly attached to the 360 camera. Additionally, we report the results of an open research challenge evaluating the best visual SLAM and localization systems from around the world. The Challenge attracted substantially higher participation in SLAM, with 62 teams compared to 22 in floor-plan-referenced localization, reflecting the broader maturity of SLAM methods. The higher errors in localization further highlight the difficulty of this task in construction and point to the need for continued research, which this dataset is intended to support. The dataset and the benchmark are publicly available at: https://hilti-trimble-challenge.com/dataset-2026.

</details>


### [290] [Clustering-Embedded Model Predictive Path Integral Control: Avoiding Averaging-Induced Failure and Enabling Efficient Cluster Selection for Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06499)
*Zidong Liu,Kaixin Chang,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CE-MPPI通过在标准MPPI中嵌入DBSCAN聚类与几何方向特征提取，解决了非凸环境中平均化导致的失效问题，在2D仿真和UR5e机械臂实验中分别实现了48%的时间节约和12%的路径缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPPI在杂乱的避障场景中，重要性加权更新会将不兼容的轨迹平均在一起，导致在障碍物正前方产生犹豫甚至碰撞，这一平均化引起的失效问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出CE-MPPI框架：1) 利用DBSCAN进行高保真轨迹剪枝与聚类；2) 从碰撞导引参考点提取几何方向特征；3) 在静态场景中最小化代价，在动态场景中主动转向避开障碍物通量。

Result: 2D仿真中缓解了障碍物前犹豫和动态场景中与移动障碍物的持续耦合；UR5e机械臂实物实验实现时间减少48%，末端路径缩短12%。

Conclusion: CE-MPPI从架构层面重新定义了控制律，有效解决了标准MPPI在非凸环境中的平均化失效问题，显著提升了规划性能与安全性。

Abstract: With the widespread availability of parallel computing hardware, sampling-based motion planning methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control have become increasingly powerful for complex nonlinear systems in non-smooth task spaces. However, the sampling and forward-simulation pipeline in MPPI suffers from averaging-induced failure in cluttered environments, where the importance-weighted update averages incompatible rollouts and leads to hesitation or even collision when an obstacle lies directly ahead. This paper proposes Clustering-Embedded MPPI (CE-MPPI), a framework that architecturally resolves the averaging-induced failures inherent in standard MPPI within non-convex environments. Rather than simply mitigating interference, CE-MPPI redefines the control law by integrating a high-fidelity pruning and clustering stage. By leveraging density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) alongside a novel geometric direction feature that is extracted from collision-derived reference points, the system isolates feasible trajectory modes from the noise of infeasible rollouts. This is paired with an intelligent selection logic that optimizes for minimum cost in static scenes while actively steering opposite to obstacle flux in dynamic environments. Experiments in 2-D JAX-accelerated simulations show that CE-MPPI alleviates obstacle-front hesitation and avoids persistent coupling with moving obstacles in dynamic scenes. In particular, real-world tests on a 6-DoF UR5e manipulator with CUDA-parallel rollouts in Isaac Gym achieve a 48\% reduction in time-to-goal and a 12\% shorter end-effector path.

</details>


### [291] [Hypothesis-driven Model Expansion under Uncertainty for Open-World Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06501)
*Anxing Xiao,Hanbo Zhang,Tianrun Hu,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向开放世界的机器人规划框架，通过结合基础模型和自动规划，使机器人能够在未知环境中自主生成、验证和更新抽象世界模型假设，实现知识自动扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统封闭世界方法依赖预编程知识库，当服务机器人在人类环境中遇到意外情况和任务时无法应对，因此需要一种能够自主扩展知识的开放世界规划方法。

Method: 提出开放世界规划框架：1）利用基础模型生成关于状态和转移的初始假设；2）应用自动规划生成同时满足假设验证和任务执行的行动序列；3）通过迭代执行和精化，将基础模型的验证反馈纳入知识更新。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，该框架实现了自主知识扩展，并能在开放世界场景中有效运作。

Conclusion: 将来自机器人基础模型的不确定性感知模型扩展与规划相结合，能够推进家用服务机器人的实际部署。

Abstract: We consider an open-world planning setting in which service robots must operate in unknown environments with incomplete knowledge of objects and actions. Traditional closed-world approaches with pre-programmed knowledge bases fail when robots encounter unexpected situations and tasks, posing a fundamental challenge for autonomous knowledge expansion in human environments. In this work, we propose an open-world planning framework that enables robots to automatically generate, verify, and update hypotheses about their abstract world models. Our key insight is to explicitly maintain uncertainty-aware knowledge expansion and integrate hypothesis verification into goal-reaching planning. The framework leverages foundation models to generate initial hypotheses over states and transitions, and applies automated planning to produce action sequences that jointly address hypothesis verification and task execution. Through iterative execution and refinement, the robot expands its knowledge by incorporating verification feedback from the foundation models when hypotheses prove incorrect. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that our framework enables autonomous knowledge expansion and effective operation in open-world settings. These results indicate that integrating uncertainty-aware model expansion from robot foundation models with planning advances the practical deployment of household service robots.

</details>


### [292] [Neural-ESO: A Dual-Pathway Architecture for Provably Robust Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06535)
*Fan Zhang,Richie Suganda,Jinfeng Chen,Wenhua Liu,Hantao Fu,Bin Hu,Qin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经扩展状态观测器（Neural-ESO）的学习型抗干扰框架，采用双路径架构（预测路径+校正路径），通过Lyapunov理论和小增益分析证明 Lipschitz 约束可保证闭环误差一致最终有界，在四旋翼着陆任务中验证了精度-鲁棒性权衡及更高的运行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的控制方法在部署后过度依赖已学习的模型，缺乏对预测误差的补偿机制，导致鲁棒性不足。本文旨在设计一种结合神经网络学习能力与传统ESO校正能力的抗干扰框架，实现精度与鲁棒性的平衡。

Method: 提出Neural-ESO双路径架构：1）预测路径（神经网络）提供前馈扰动估计以加速收敛；2）校正路径（传统ESO）补偿预测误差，防止对神经组分的过度依赖。通过Lyapunov理论和小增益分析，证明对学习组件施加Lipschitz界可保证闭环误差动力学的一致最终有界性。

Result: 在四旋翼着陆任务（含强烈地面效应扰动）的正常和分布外场景中验证，相比SOTA基线，Neural-ESO在训练、部署和迁移阶段均展现出更好的精度-鲁棒性权衡及更高的运行可靠性。

Conclusion: Neural-ESO通过双路径架构有效融合了学习方法的快速收敛能力与传统ESO的鲁棒补偿能力，Lipschitz约束为学习组件的稳定性提供了理论保障，该方法在复杂扰动环境下具有显著优势。

Abstract: A learning-enabled disturbance-rejection framework based on a Neural Extended State Observer (Neural-ESO) is presented in this letter. Unlike existing learning-based control methods that largely rely on the learned model once deployed, Neural-ESO adopts a dual-pathway architecture: a predictive pathway uses a neural network to provide a feedforward disturbance estimate that accelerates convergence, while a corrective pathway employs a conventional ESO to compensate prediction errors and prevent over-reliance on the neural component. Using Lyapunov theory and a small-gain analysis, we show that enforcing a Lipschitz bound on the learning component guarantees uniform ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop error dynamics. The proposed framework is validated on a quadrotor landing task subject to strong ground-effect disturbances across normal and out-of-distribution scenarios, demonstrating accuracy-robustness trade-off and greater operational reliability during training, deployment, and transfer compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [293] [UniLM-Nav: A Unified Framework for Zero-Shot Last-Mile Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06537)
*Zhuofan Zhang,Tianxu Wang,Guoxi Zhang,Yixiong Lin,Xilin Wang,Hongming Xu,Qing Li,Song-Chun Zhu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLM-Nav是一个统一的零样本开放词汇最后一米导航框架，通过共享的多模态大语言模型后端，将最后一米导航分解为视图选择、任务条件化可通行性定位和几何感知基座姿态推理，在OVMM基准上比SOTA方法MoTo高出3.13个百分点，并在真实四足机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最后一米导航方法依赖人工姿态标注或任务特定训练，难以扩展到具有细粒度空间约束的开放词汇场景，机器人到达目标附近后无法保证获得适合操作的基座姿态。

Method: 提出UniLM-Nav统一框架，将最后一米导航分解为三个步骤：(1) 视图选择——从最近采集的观测中选择最佳捕捉目标物体或容器的参考视图；(2) 任务条件化可通行性定位——在选定视图中定位任务相关可通行点；(3) 几何感知基座姿态推理——结合可通行点、任务上下文和机器人几何信息推断适用于操作的基座姿态。三个步骤共享同一个MLLM后端完成。

Result: 在OVMM基准上，UniLM-Nav超越之前SOTA方法MoTo，提升了3.13个百分点。消融实验表明各组件对最终性能至关重要，MLLM的选择也有显著影响。在搭载6自由度Z1机械臂的Unitree B2四足机器人上进行了实际部署验证。

Conclusion: UniLM-Nav通过MLLM统一解决最后一米导航问题，无需额外训练或人工标注即可实现开放词汇场景下的零样本操作，是一种可扩展且适用于真实移动操作任务的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to navigate to a target object or receptacle and then perform intended manipulation. However, reaching the vicinity of the target does not guarantee a manipulation-ready base pose, a problem known as last-mile navigation. Prior methods for last-mile navigation either rely on manual pose annotation or task-specific training, limiting their scalability to open-vocabulary settings with fine-grained spatial constraints. We propose UniLM-Nav, a unified framework for zero-shot open-vocabulary last-mile navigation. UniLM-Nav decomposes last-mile navigation into view selection, task-conditioned affordance grounding, and geometry-aware base-pose reasoning, all resolved with a shared multimodal large language model (MLLM) backend. Specifically, UniLM-Nav first selects a reference view that best captures the target object or receptacle from recently collected observations. It then grounds task-relevant affordance point in the selected view and lifts the result into the robot-centric coordinate frame. Finally, conditioned on the grounded affordance, task context, and robot geometry, it infers a manipulation-ready base pose for the robot. We evaluate UniLM-Nav on the OVMM benchmark, where it outperforms the previous state-of-the-art method, MoTo, by 3.13 percentage points. Analyses show that the components of our method are crucial to final performance, and that the choice of MLLM also has a substantial effect. We further deploy UniLM-Nav on a Unitree B2 quadruped robot with a 6-DoF Unitree Z1 manipulator, validating its applicability to real-world mobile manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [294] [RynnWorld-Teleop: An Action-Conditioned World Model for Digital Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06558)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Hangyu Li,Biao Gong,Kehan Li,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出数字遥操作范式RynnWorld-Teleop，用生成式世界模型替代物理机器人，通过手部姿态驱动生成高保真视频，实现与硬件解耦的大规模机器人数据采集，训练的策略可零样本迁移到真实灵巧双手任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理遥操作数据采集受限于操作者时间、具体硬件和工作空间的绑定，成为规模化机器人学习的瓶颈，亟需一种与硬件解耦、可扩展的数据收集方式。

Method: 提出数字遥操作范式：1）操作者手部姿态流驱动以机器人为中心的生成世界模型，从单张参考图像合成高保真第一人称视频；2）记录姿态流作为与具身无关的动作标签，通过标准重定向迁移到任意目标机器人；3）系统RynnWorld-Teleop集成深度感知骨骼条件化、渐进式人-机器人视频扩散Transformer训练、流式自回归蒸馏，实现单次推理40+ FPS的实时交互生成。

Result: 仅用RynnWorld-Teleop生成数据训练的策略，在灵巧和多样化双手任务上实现了有效的零样本Sim2Real迁移；将该数字遥操作数据增强真实数据集后，持续提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: RynnWorld-Teleop作为一种高保真、可扩展的数据引擎，能够有效支持下一代机器人智能体的训练，验证了数字遥操作范式在规模化机器人学习中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Scaling robot learning requires massive, diverse trajectory data, yet collection is currently bottlenecked by physical teleoperation, where every demonstration binds operator time to specific hardware and workspaces. We introduce digital teleoperation, a paradigm that decouples data collection from physical constraints by replacing the real robot with a generative world model. In this framework, an operator's hand-pose stream drives a robot-centric generative world model to synthesize high-fidelity egocentric videos from a single reference image. The recorded pose stream serves as an embodiment-agnostic action label transferable to any target robot via standard retargeting, yielding complete state-action trajectories for imitation learning independent of physical hardware. We instantiate this paradigm in RynnWorld-Teleop, a system that integrates depth-aware skeletal conditioning, progressive human-to-robot training on a video Diffusion Transformer, and streaming autoregressive distillation. This pipeline compresses the generative process into a single-pass inference, enabling 40+ FPS, real-time interactive generation on a single H100 GPU. Policies trained exclusively on RynnWorld-Teleop-generated data achieve effective zero-shot Sim2Real transfer across dexterous and diverse bimanual tasks. Moreover, augmenting real-world datasets with our digitally teleoperated data consistently improves success rates, demonstrating that RynnWorld-Teleop serves as a high-fidelity, scalable data engine for the next generation of robotic agents.

</details>


### [295] [RynnWorld-4D: 4D Embodied World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06559)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RynnWorld-4D，一种联合生成未来RGB帧、深度图和光流的4D世界模型，通过RGB-DF多模态协同表示缩小世界预测与策略学习之间的差距；并在此基础上构建RynnWorld-4D-Policy，利用逆向动力学头直接从4D内部表征输出机器人动作，在真实世界灵巧双臂操作任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界中的机器人操作不仅需要识别场景外观，还需要预测场景3D结构在交互下的动态变化。现有的2D像素视频表示与机器人所需的低层级末端执行器动作之间存在较大鸿沟，因此需要一种能够统一外观、几何与运动信息的物理化4D表示来缩小世界预测与策略学习之间的差距。

Method: 1) 提出RGB-DF（RGB+深度+光流）多模态协同表示，融合视觉外观、几何结构与时序运动；2) 构建RynnWorld-4D生成模型，采用三分支架构与跨模态注意力、帧级3D RoPE，从单张RGB-D图像和语言指令统一扩散生成未来RGB、深度和光流；3) 构建大规模数据集Rynn4DDataset 1.0（超2.54亿帧），含伪标签深度和光流；4) 提出RynnWorld-4D-Policy，通过逆向动力学头单次前向传播直接利用4D内部表征输出动作，免去多步去噪。

Result: RynnWorld-4D能生成时空一致的4D预测；RynnWorld-4D-Policy在真实世界灵巧双臂操作任务上达到SOTA性能，尤其在需要空间精度和时间协调的任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: RGB-DF多模态协同表示能够有效捕捉场景的4D动态，统一扩散框架下联合生成RGB、深度和光流是可行的；基于4D内部表征的逆向动力学策略学习方法能够高效输出机器人动作，显著提升灵巧操作任务的表现。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in the open world requires not only recognizing what a scene looks like, but also anticipating how its 3D structure moves under interaction. We argue that synchronized RGB, depth, and optical flow, namely RGB-DF, provide a physically grounded representation that captures the underlying 4D dynamics of a scene. Compared to 2D pixel videos, this multi-modal synergy aligns visual appearance with geometric structure and temporal motion, creating a representation space significantly closer to the low-level end-effector actions demanded by robotic systems, thereby narrowing the gap between world prediction and policy learning. Building on this insight, we introduce RynnWorld-4D, a generative model that co-produces future RGB frames, depth maps, and optical flow from a single RGB-D image and a language instruction within one unified diffusion process. This 4D world model features a tri-branch architecture that integrates cross-modal attention with frame-wise 3D RoPE, ensuring that appearance, geometry, and motion evolve consistently. To supply training data at scale, we curate Rynn4DDataset 1.0, a massive dataset of over 254.4 million frames across egocentric human and robotic manipulation videos with high-quality pseudo-labels for depth and optical flow. We further propose RynnWorld-4D-Policy, an inverse dynamics head that consumes the internal 4D representations of RynnWorld-4D in a single forward pass, bypassing expensive multi-step denoising, to output robot actions in a closed-loop manner. Experiments show that RynnWorld-4D produces temporally and spatially coherent 4D predictions, and that RynnWorld-4D-Policy achieves state-of-the-art performance on real-world dexterous bimanual manipulation tasks, particularly excelling in tasks demanding spatial precision and temporal coordination.

</details>


### [296] [Embodied Human-Robot Interaction via Acoustics: A MARL Approach with AcoustoBots for Spatial Data Physicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06563)
*Shiqi Liu,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Prateek Mittal,Pengyuan Wei,Sriram Subramanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AcoustoBots——一种移动声悬浮数据物理化平台。TurtleBot3机器人搭载超声相控阵，利用声悬浮粒子高度（1-10 cm）编码城市标量数据（如人口密度、噪声、交通）。基于MADDPG的多智能体强化学习策略实现碰撞感知导航，GS-PAT声控制器维持悬浮稳定性。在4m×3m缩比英国地图上验证，单/双机器人任务成功率分别达90%/80%，实现运动中稳定悬浮和位置相关高度渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据物理化通常静态且脱离真实环境，难以传达具身空间动态和吸引用户参与。本文旨在解决这一局限，通过移动声悬浮平台实现动态、空间感知的数据物理化。

Method: 采用TurtleBot3机器人搭载8×8超声相控阵，粒子悬浮高度（1-10 cm）编码城市标量值。使用基于MADDPG的MARL策略进行集中训练/分散执行的碰撞感知导航，GS-PAT声控制器维持运动中的陷阱稳定性。构建闭环感知-显示-动作系统。

Result: 在4m×3m缩比UK地图上，单机器人任务成功率90%，双机器人协作覆盖率80%（各10次试验），碰撞次数低。实现了运动中稳定悬浮和位置依赖的高度一致渲染。

Conclusion: 声悬浮技术可作为简单、可一瞥即懂的机器人介导通信线索，用于具身人机交互中的空间数据分析。

Abstract: Traditional data physicalization is often static and disconnected from real environments, limiting its ability to convey embodied spatial dynamics and engage users. To address this limitation, we present AcoustoBots, a mobile acoustophoretic data-physicalization platform in which TurtleBot3 robots carry upward-facing 8 x 8 ultrasonic phased arrays. Each array levitates a particle whose height (1-10 cm) encodes a local urban scalar value, such as population density, noise, or traffic. A MARL (Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning) policy based on the Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) algorithm, with centralized training and decentralized execution, selects collision-aware navigation actions, while a high-rate Gerchberg-Saxton-Phased Array of Transducers (GS-PAT) acoustic controller maintains trap stability and updates array phases to achieve the commanded height during motion. This creates a closed perception-display-action loop. We evaluate single-robot city-to-city traversal and dual-robot cooperative coverage on a 4 m x 3 m scaled UK map using PhaseSpace-based localization for repeatable multi-robot trials. Results show stable in-motion levitation and consistent, location-dependent height rendering, with task success rates of 90% and 80% for the single and dual-robot regimes, respectively, over 10 trials per regime, and low collision counts. These findings support acoustophoretic levitation as a simple, glanceable, robot-mediated communication cue for embodied human-robot interaction in spatial analytics.

</details>


### [297] [Lift3D-VLA: Lifting VLA Models to 3D Geometry and Dynamics-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06564)
*Jiaming Liu,Qingpo Wuwu,Nuowei Han,Hao Chen,Zhuoyang Liu,Fan Fei,Yueru Jia,Chenyang Gu,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Lift3D-VLA框架，通过显式3D点云推理、几何中心遮罩自编码（GC-MAE）和分层时序动作建模，解决了VLA模型在几何理解与动态环境时序动作生成上的不足，在22个模拟和8个真实任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽具备强泛化能力，但在物理机器人操作中缺乏几何理解与空间推理能力；已有引入3D信息的方法受限于数据稀缺和编码过程中的几何信息丢失，且无法在动态环境中联合捕捉3D几何与时间结构化动作。

Method: 1) 基于Lift3D的改进2D模型提升策略，实现3D点与预训练2D位置嵌入的几何对齐，直接编码点云到VLA视觉编码器；2) 提出GC-MAE双目标自监督框架，同时重建当前点云并预测其未来几何演变；3) 设计分层时序动作建模，利用LLM多层协作预测动作块，保证时序一致性。

Result: 在MetaWorld和RLBench上分别比最优VLA方法平均成功率提高10.8%和11.1%；真实世界中比最强基线高出4个百分点；对分布外扰动具更强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式3D点云推理与自监督几何动力学学习能有效增强VLA模型的空间理解与时序动作生成能力，为机器人操作提供更具泛化性和鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization across diverse tasks. However, effective robotic manipulation in physical environments fundamentally requires geometric understanding and spatial reasoning. While some VLA approaches attempt to incorporate 3D information, they are constrained by limited data availability and geometric information loss in current 3D encoding pipelines, and fail to jointly capture 3D geometry and temporally structured actions in dynamic environments. To address these limitations, we introduce Lift3D-VLA, a unified VLA framework that equips models with explicit 3D point cloud reasoning and enables temporally coherent action generation. First, building upon our previous work Lift3D, an enhanced 2D model-lifting strategy is proposed to geometrically align 3D points with pretrained 2D positional embeddings. This design enables direct point-cloud encoding within the VLA vision encoder while minimizing spatial information loss. Based on explicit 3D inputs, we propose Geometry-Centric Masked Autoencoding (GC-MAE), a dual-objective self-supervised framework that reconstructs the current point cloud while predicting its future geometric evolution. This formulation allows the 2D vision encoder to internalize both 3D structure and physical dynamics. To fully exploit 3D representations, we further design layer-wise temporal action modeling, which leverages multiple layers of the LLM to collaboratively predict action chunks, enabling temporally consistent predictions. Across 22 simulated tasks and 8 real-world manipulation tasks, Lift3D-VLA achieves 10.8% and 11.1% higher mean success rates on MetaWorld and RLBench than the best-performing prior VLA methods, and outperforms the strongest real-world baseline by 4 percentage points, while exhibiting stronger generalization to out-of-distribution perturbations.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [AIED's Unfinished Mission: Centering Agency and Motivation in the Age of Effortless Bypass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05557)
*H. Chad Lane*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 随着通用AI可完成复杂认知任务，学习者面临"轻松绕过困境"——选择费力投入还是走捷径。本文主张AIED应在继续构建高效智能教育工具的同时，着重确保学习者选择真实投入，并从动机和学习理论出发提出五个发展方向和四种愿景技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用AI的广泛可及性使学习者能够轻松绕过学习任务中的认知劳动，这对教育系统构成大规模威胁。AIED领域必须直面这一"轻松绕过困境"，确保学习者在有更轻松替代方案时仍选择费力的真实投入。

Method: 基于既有动机理论和学习理论，提出五个发展方向：支持自主性与能动性、培养学习者对元认知威胁的韧性、设计兴趣与相关性、强化过程性评估、赋能教师。随后分享四种体现上述特征的愿景技术。

Result: 提出了五个AIED应对"轻松绕过困境"的发展方向，以及四种愿景技术作为未来系统设计的示范。

Conclusion: AIED需要继续构建高效智能教育工具，但必须将确保学习者选择真实投入作为核心紧迫任务，推动领域演进。

Abstract: The widespread availability of general-purpose AI that can perform complex cognitive tasks threatens to undermine education at scale. This effortless bypass dilemma sharpens a challenge AIED has long engaged with but must now confront directly: ensuring learners choose effortful engagement when easier alternatives are available to complete learning tasks. In this paper, I argue that AIED's longstanding agenda of building more effective intelligent educational tools should continue, but with a renewed emphasis on the urgency of ensuring learners choose to engage authentically. Drawing on established motivational and learning theories, I outline five directions in which AIED can build on its existing strengths: supporting autonomy and agency, building learner resilience to metacognitive threats, designing for interest and relevance, amplifying process-based assessment, and empowering teachers. I then share four envisioned technologies that embody key features of this future and conclude by outlining how AIED must now evolve.

</details>


### [299] [Collective Cognition in Hybrid Groups: A Network Science Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05593)
*Babak Hemmatian,Razan Baltaji,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文综合网络科学、集体认知与多智能体系统视角，探讨注意力、记忆与推理差异如何影响人机混合团队中的集体智能涌现，分析了从纯人类/纯AI网络到混合网络的扩展与变化，并指出混合团队中关键的结构性转变（如人类作为AI子网络的守门人）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别在个体/二元层面对人机互动有初步理解，但缺乏对群体层面动态的形式化解释；网络科学主要关注纯人类或纯AI系统，未充分探讨其结论如何推广到混合系统。

Method: 综合网络科学、集体认知和多智能体系统文献，通过注意力、记忆与推理三个认知维度进行比较分析，考察任务环境、群体拓扑、智能体层面过程和激励结构对集体结果的影响，并扩展到异质人机节点与边的混合网络设置。

Result: 识别出哪些网络效应在不同智能体类型间是稳健的、哪些需要修正；发现纯人类/纯AI传统中被边缘化的配置（如人类作为AI子网络的守门人）在混合团队中成为结构性核心；揭示了探索-利用、效率-冗余等经典权衡在混合团队中可能以不同方式运作。

Conclusion: 为混合智能系统的组织设计、治理与负责任开发提供了启示，强调了从认知系统视角整合网络科学来理解混合团队集体智能的必要性。

Abstract: The growing integration of AI agents into human teams calls for a principled understanding of how collective intelligence emerges in hybrid systems. Recent frameworks clarify how attention, memory, and reasoning differences shape human-AI interaction at the individual and dyadic levels, but a formal account of how these differences scale to group-level dynamics is lacking. Most network science has examined either human-only or multi-agent AI-only systems, leaving open how its findings and parametrizations translate to hybrid groups. This chapter synthesizes network science, collective cognition, and multi-agent systems through the lens of attention, memory, and reasoning. We review how task environments, group topologies, agent-level processes, and incentive structures shape collective outcomes in human-only and AI-only networks, then examine how these results extend to hybrid settings, conceptualizing hybrid networks as heterogeneous human-AI nodes and links with distinct individual and transactive constraints. Our comparative analysis identifies which network effects are robust across agent types and which require revision, and highlights configurations that were peripheral in single-type traditions, such as human gatekeepers of AI sub-networks, but become structurally central in hybrid teams. Integrating a cognitive systems perspective with network science, we clarify how established exploration-exploitation and efficiency-redundancy trade-offs may operate differently in hybrid teams, and conclude with implications for organizational design, governance, and the responsible development of hybrid intelligence systems.

</details>


### [300] [GeoXplain: On-the-Fly Visual Explanations for Weather Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05655)
*Clemens Walter Koprolin,Leonardo Trentini,Benedikt Soja,Mennatallah El-Assady,Christina Humer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GeoXplain是一个交互式Python可视化工具包，用于探索地球科学基础模型（如Aurora）生成的高维天气预报归因图，支持地图/地球仪模式、时间线、气压层控制等功能，可pip安装且开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气和气候基础模型生成的高维预测结果中，学习到的变量间关系难以通过静态图进行有效检查，需要交互式可视化工具来探索归因图。

Method: 开发了一个基于Python的交互式可视化工具包GeoXplain，接受包含归因网格及相关元数据的归因包，在笔记本widget或浏览器中渲染；同时设计了GeoXplain Aurora Adapter作为首个计算后端，支持多种归因方法（梯度显著性、Integrated Gradients、RISE、ViT-CX等）并兼容本地GPU、GPU监听器或SLURM后端。

Result: GeoXplain提供了地图和地球仪两种模式、关联时间线、气压层控制、目标标注及物理场叠加等可视化功能；Aurora Adapter支持多种归因计算方法，可作为PyPI包安装。

Conclusion: GeoXplain是一个模型无关的地球系统可视化工具包，通过交互式归因图探索提升了气候基础模型的可解释性，首个计算后端适配了Aurora模型。

Abstract: Weather and climate foundation models produce high-dimensional forecasts whose learned relationships are difficult to inspect with static plots alone. GeoXplain is an interactive Python-based visualization toolkit for exploring geospatial attribution maps across climate variables, atmospheric pressure levels, and forecast time. The toolkit accepts attribution bundles containing attribution grids together with corresponding metadata and renders them in a notebook widget or browser with map and globe modes, linked timelines, pressure-level controls, target annotations, and optional physical-field overlays. We frame GeoXplain as a model-agnostic earth-system visualization toolkit and present the GeoXplain Aurora Adapter as its first computation backend. The adapter computes explanations for the Aurora foundation model, either in a local GPU process, through a GPU listener, or through a SLURM-backed listener, while preserving the same Python call site for analysts. It currently supports gradient saliency, Integrated Gradients, RISE, ViT-CX, multi-frame saliency and Integrated Gradients rollouts, and retrieval of ERA5 overlays. GeoXplain can be installed as a PyPI package with pip install geoxplain. The code is open-source and available at https://github.com/clemenskoprolin/geoxplain.

</details>


### [301] [Perceived System Predictability: Scale Development and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05674)
*Hendrik Schuff,Heike Adel,Ngoc Thang Vu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出感知系统可预测性(PSP)作为以用户为中心的构念，基于不确定性理论区分了认知性、偶然性和有效性可预测性，开发并验证了6项PSP量表，通过两个实验(N=200)证明PSP与预测正确性、信任等概念存在差异，为设计透明可信的交互系统提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互领域缺乏对用户感知系统可预测性的精确概念化和有效测量工具，而这一感知直接影响用户如何解释、信任和依赖系统。

Method: 基于不确定性理论构建PSP理论框架，从60题项池经专家评审和认知访谈提炼出6项PSP量表，通过形状分类器研究(N=200)验证量表单维与三因子分层结构，再通过情感分类器研究(N=200)操纵解释和随机性，考察PSP与预测正确性、信任、主观信息处理意识、认知需求的关系。

Result: PSP与预测正确性捕捉用户心理模型的不同方面且两者可能背离：PSP本身预测正确性，解释改变PSP但不改变正确性，增加随机性降低正确性但不降低PSP。

Conclusion: PSP超越了现有的客观和主观测量指标，为设计透明可信的交互系统提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: How predictable users perceive an interactive system to be shapes how they interpret, trust, and rely on it, yet HCI lacks both a precise conceptualization and a validated instrument for this perception. We address this gap by introducing perceived system predictability (PSP) as a user-centered construct grounded in uncertainty theory, distinguishing epistemic, aleatory, and effective predictability. We contribute (i) a theoretical framework that situates PSP relative to adjacent constructs such as trust and understanding, (ii) a 6-item PSP scale, derived from a 60-item pool through expert review and cognitive interviews, and validated in a shape-classifier study ($N=200$) that supports both a unidimensional and a three-factor hierarchical structure, and (iii) a sentiment-classifier study ($N=200$) that varies explanations and stochasticity, and relates PSP to the correctness of users' predictions of system behavior, trust, subjective information processing awareness, and need for cognition. We find that PSP and prediction correctness capture distinct aspects of users' mental models and that both can diverge: PSP itself predicts correctness, explanations shift PSP but not correctness, and increased stochasticity degrades correctness without lowering PSP. PSP thus goes beyond existing objective and subjective measures and offers a principled foundation for designing transparent and trustworthy interactive systems.

</details>


### [302] [Depression Symptoms and Relational Patterns in 187k ChatGPT Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05685)
*Neil K. R. Sehgal,Dunigan Folk,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究分析了766名PHQ-8评分参与者的187,093条ChatGPT对话，发现高抑郁症状（PHQ≥10）用户更频繁地使用ChatGPT进行心理健康、人际、孤独、自我关注和支持寻求类对话，尤其在深夜且有月度重复模式。其语言包含更多第一人称单数代词和绝对化术语。基于语言的筛查预测效果有限（AUROC 0.591）。研究认为，这些对话不应被视为临床筛查数据，而应视为LLM被用作非正式支持基础设施的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型越来越多地被用作私密、随时可用的对话系统，但人们对于有抑郁症状的用户如何使用这些系统知之甚少。基于CSCW在自我披露和同伴支持方面的工作，本研究旨在探索ChatGPT作为一种新兴的非正式支持基础设施的角色。

Method: 收集766名完成PHQ-8问卷参与者的187,093条ChatGPT对话数据，以中度症状阈值（10分）为界，比较低于该阈值和高于/等于该阈值的两组用户。分析对话主题、时间模式、语言特征（第一人称单数代词、绝对化术语）、披露程度和专业转介情况，并进行基于语言的预测建模。

Result: 高PHQ组用户更多使用ChatGPT进行心理健康、人际、孤独、自我关注和支持寻求类对话；具有明显的深夜使用模式和月度重复模式；语言中使用更多第一人称单数代词和绝对化术语；在高披露情境中更多使用ChatGPT，但专业转介率未显著升高；基于语言的筛查预测效果有限（AUROC 0.591）。

Conclusion: 这些对话历史不应被当作临床筛查数据来使用，而应被视为LLM日益被用作非正式支持基础设施的证据。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as private, always-available conversational systems, but little is known about how people with depressive symptoms use them. Building on CSCW work on disclosure and peer support, we examine ChatGPT as an emerging informal support infrastructure: private, persistent, responsive, and available outside ordinary hours. We analyze 187,093 ChatGPT conversations from 766 participants who completed the PHQ-8, comparing those below the moderate-symptom threshold (score of 10) with those at or above it. Higher-PHQ participants used ChatGPT more for mental-health, interpersonal, loneliness, self-focused, and support-seeking conversations, with pronounced late-night and recurring month-level patterns. Their language contained more first-person singular pronouns and absolutist terms. They more often engaged ChatGPT in high-disclosure contexts, but professional redirection was not higher. Language-based prediction was modest and insufficient for screening (AUROC 0.591). We argue these histories should not be treated as clinical screening data but as evidence LLMs are increasingly used as informal support infrastructure.

</details>


### [303] [Plainbook: Data Science, in Plain Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05717)
*Luca de Alfaro,Mathis Aubert,Ranjit Jhala,Eliana Pastor,Elena Baralis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Plainbook是一种以自然语言为中心的计算笔记本，通过自动代码生成、线性执行语义和值验证机制，使不熟悉编程的科学家也能进行可复现的计算分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jupyter Notebook虽然在数据科学中被广泛用于共享可复现的计算分析，但只有理解代码的人才能使用。为了让更广泛的、不熟悉代码但对数据分析和计算感兴趣的科学家群体也能使用计算笔记本，作者提出了Plainbook。

Method: Plainbook基于两个原则：1) 提升自然语言描述的地位，保留自然语言描述而非结果代码，代码从单元格描述中自动生成；2) 验证值的正确性。采用线性执行语义（保证单元格按顺序执行，避免Jupyter中的"隐藏状态"或乱序执行问题），并内置了基于值和值检查的验证机制（包括类似单元测试的单个单元格验证和全局验证），所有这些由快照内核支撑以缓存执行状态，提高执行和验证效率。

Result: Plainbook实现了让不熟悉代码的用户也能进行计算分析的核心目标，通过自然语言驱动、线性执行和内置验证机制解决了Jupyter Notebook在可访问性和正确性验证方面的关键问题。

Conclusion: Plainbook通过以自然语言替代代码为核心的计算笔记本范式，降低了计算分析的门槛，同时通过线性执行语义和值验证机制确保了计算的可复现性和正确性，为更广泛的科学研究者提供了一种更易用的计算分析工具。

Abstract: Jupyter Notebooks have become widely adopted in data science, as they allow the sharing of reproducible computational analysis. They are, however, accessible only to people who understand computer code. To reach the broader audience of scientists interested in data analysis and computation, but unfamiliar with code, we introduce Plainbook, notebooks centered on natural language rather than code.
  Plainbook is based on two principles: promote the natural language descriptions, and verify the values. In plainbook, the natural language descriptions are preserved, rather than the resulting code; the code is generated automatically from the cell descriptions. As natural language is read top to bottom, Plainbook adopts a linear execution semantics, in which cells are guaranteed to be executed in the order in which they appear; there is no "hidden state" or out-of-order execution as in Jupyter. To allow users who may not understand code to verify the correctness of the computation, we have built into Plainbook verification mechanisms centered on values and value inspection. These include mechanisms that focus on individual cells, akin to unit tests, as well as global mechanisms. Both the linear execution semantics, and the verification mechanisms, are underpinned by a snapshot kernel that caches execution states and makes execution and verification efficient.

</details>


### [304] [PERSONAJUDGE: Simulating Individual Human Preference Judgments with Evaluator-Specific Demonstration Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05742)
*Zeyu He,Xuan Qi,Subramanian Chidambaram,Zhichao Xu,Vinayak Arannil,Lydia Chilton,Alex C. Williams*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合分类判断与评估者辅助数据（推理轨迹和接口遥测）的LLM仿真方法，通过上下文学习模拟个体评估者。在32名标注者、4200个偏好判断的实验中，该方法最高提升9.9个百分点；推理轨迹贡献最大但采集成本高，接口遥测反而损害性能；仿真难度受评估者的中立倾向和与共识的偏离程度预测，其中中立倾向是跨任务稳定的属性（r=0.728）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作为评估者的方法依赖共识偏好，忽略了不同评估者之间的个体差异，无法实现个性化的AI评估。

Method: 提出一种基于上下文学习的仿真方法，将分类判断与评估者特有的辅助数据（回顾性推理轨迹和接口遥测）相结合，通过32名训练标注者在4×4×4因子设计下的4200个偏好判断进行系统实证研究。

Result: (1) 仿真方法相比基线评估器最高提升9.9个百分点；(2) 推理轨迹收益最大但采集成本高，接口遥测反而损害性能；(3) 评估者的中立倾向（尤其是在Helpfulness维度）和与共识的偏离程度可预测仿真难度，其中中立倾向是跨任务稳定的属性（r=0.728）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了评估者特有辅助数据在个性化评估中的潜力与局限性，为规模化个体感知的AI评估提供了方法论洞见。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as judges in AI evaluation, but current approaches rely on consensus preferences that ignore individual evaluator variation. We propose a novel simulation approach that combines categorical judgments with evaluator-specific auxiliary data--retrospective reasoning traces and interface telemetry--to enable LLM-based simulation of individual evaluators via in-context learning. We conduct a systematic empirical study of this approach using multi-facet data from 32 trained annotators across 4,200 preference judgments in a 4 x 4 x 4 factorial design. Our key findings: (1) The simulation approach achieves up to 9.9 percentage point improvements over the Base Judge; (2) Reasoning traces provide the largest gains with higher collection efforts, while interface telemetry often hurts rather than helps performance despite being cheaper to collect. (3) Simulation difficulty is systematic, predicted by an evaluator's neutral usage (most clearly on Helpfulness) and divergence from consensus; the neutral-usage tendency--rather than simulatability itself--is the cross-task-stable property (r = 0.728). These results establish both the potential and limits of evaluator-specific auxiliary data for personalized evaluation, offering methodological insights for scaling individual aware AI assessment.

</details>


### [305] [VisTCP: A Visualization Framework to Construct Knowledge-Graph-Based Representation for Traditional Chinese Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05841)
*Zhiguang Zhou,Fengling Zheng,Miaoxin Hu,Lina You,Jin Wen,Huan Liu,Wei Zhang,Dekun Qian,Yuhua Liu,Wei Chen,Yigang Wang,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VisTCP是一个结合TCP智能模型与专家知识的可视化框架，通过人机协同方式实现中国传统绘画的可信结构化表征，从而支持考古与艺术史研究中的语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像结构化表征方法在中国传统绘画上表现不佳，原因包括：1) TCP中的物体和事件与现代自然图像差异显著，导致语义误解；2) 即使是领域专家也难以准确识别TCP中的古代物体和事件。因此需要一种针对TCP的结构化表征方法。

Method: 首先通过三位领域专家的预研究构建TCP语义分类体系；然后用专家标注数据训练TCP导向的结构化表征模型；设计联合嵌入可视化视图展示专家标注与模型预测之间的差异，让用户基于领域知识细化结构化表征，实现模型的迭代优化。

Result: 通过真实数据集上的案例研究、使用场景和专家访谈，验证了VisTCP在支持TCP结构化表征和语义理解方面的有效性。

Conclusion: VisTCP通过人机协同的方式，有效结合了TCP智能模型和专家知识，实现了对中国传统绘画的可信结构化表征和语义理解，为考古和艺术史研究提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Structured representation can characterize semantic objects and relationships in images. It provides a possible effective way for the semantic understanding of Traditional Chinese Paintings (TCPs) to better support archaeology and art history research. However, most image-oriented structured representation methods perform poorly on TCPs, due to two major challenges: 1) the objects and events of TCPs exhibit substantial differences from modern natural images, which results in semantic misunderstandings of TCPs; and 2) it is difficult to achieve accurate identification of ancient objects and events in TCPs, even for domain experts.In this paper, we propose VisTCP, a visualization framework that combines a TCP-oriented intelligent model and expert knowledge, which enables art historians to achieve trustworthy structured representations of TCPs in a human-in-the-loop manner. Firstly, we conduct a pilot study with three domain experts to build a semantic taxonomy of TCPs. Then, expert-annotated data are used to train a TCP-oriented structured representation model, which can automatically extract meaningful objects and their relationships in TCPs. To inform users of the model uncertainty, we design a joint embedding visualization view to show the differences between expert annotations and model predictions. This allows users to refine the structured representation based on their domain knowledge, enabling iterative optimization of the model. Finally, we conduct a case study, a usage scenario, and expert interviews on a real dataset to demonstrate the effectiveness of VisTCP in supporting the structured representation and semantic understanding of TCPs.

</details>


### [306] [Nested Episodic State Topology (NEST): A Graph-Theoretic Architecture of Cognitive States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06055)
*Ishant*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NEST提出了一种基于图论的认知表征本体，将认知建模为结构化状态的形成与转化，而非完成时的经验模型。核心包括六类关系边、信念图与工作记忆图分离、操作符工具包，以及将ACT-R、Soar、Sigma等多种认知架构映射为同一语言下的约束区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知架构（如ACT-R、Soar等）各自采用不同的表征假设，缺乏统一的、基础性的表征本体来支持跨理论比较和集成。作者旨在提供一个透明的、图论驱动的底层表征基质，使得不同认知理论可以作为该语言下的特例被表达和约束。

Method: 采用图论形式化方法，定义：(1) 带类型、带权重的图表示概念、情节、知觉和任务上下文；(2) 六类关系边（因果、包含、时间、关联、证据、空间）；(3) 耐久信念图与容量有限的工作记忆图分离；(4) WM-信念接地、冲突目录和信念更新算子；(5) 可复用操作符工具包（激活、图-性质泛函、WM转换、觉知与轨迹泛函、信念更新）；(6) 派生诊断指标（碎片化、参与度、符号评估、连贯性、主动冲突）；(7) 自相关的自我图像子图建模。

Result: 完成了：(1) 现象签名（phenomena signatures）的实例化；(2) 任务实例化模式（动作选择与失败模式）；(3) 兼容性映射，将ACT-R、Soar、Sigma、Common Model of Cognition、全局工作空间理论、语义网络、Theory-Theory以及chunking嵌入为同一语言下的约束区域。

Conclusion: 贡献是基础性的：提供了一个透明的表征基质，供后续的经验性、计算性和领域特定性研究使用。讨论了范围、局限性和未来研究方向。

Abstract: We present NEST (Nested Episodic State Topology), a foundational graph-theoretic representational ontology for modeling cognition as structured state formation and transformation rather than as a finished empirical model. Concepts, episodes, percepts, and task contexts are represented as typed, weighted graphs whose nodes may carry internal subgraph payloads; edges are typed under six relation classes -- causal, containment, temporal, associative, evidential, and spatial. Durable belief graphs are separated from capacity-limited working-memory graphs that may host transient non-belief content. WM-belief grounding, conflict catalogs, and belief-update operators specify how transient structure is tested against stored knowledge and how belief is revised. A reusable operator toolkit -- activation, graph-property functionals, working-memory transitions, awareness and trajectory functionals, and belief update -- organizes the formal core. Derived diagnostics such as fragmentation, involvement, signed evaluation, coherence, and active conflict define familiar phenomena in the same ontology; self-related processing is modeled through designated self-image subgraphs within belief. Subsequent sections instantiate this core without new primitives: phenomena signatures, a task-instantiation schema for action selection and failure modes, and compatibility mappings that embed ACT-R, Soar, Sigma, the Common Model of Cognition, Global Workspace Theory, semantic networks, Theory-Theory, and chunking as constrained regions of one language. Mappings constitute the culminating technical section; discussion addresses scope, limitations, and open research directions. The contribution is intentionally foundational: a transparent representational substrate for later empirical, computational, and domain-specific work.

</details>


### [307] [Designing Computerized Gait Analysis for Pediatric Care: Clinician Perspectives on Sensing, Workflow, and Care Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06076)
*Elizabeth Hong,Andrea Green,Ge Wang,Yiwen Dong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对12名儿科临床医生和1名系统设计师的定性研究，探讨了儿童步态分析(CGA)技术的特殊设计需求，包括应对感官敏感、身体比例适配、患者参与度维持等挑战，并提出了以儿科为中心的CGA设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机步态分析(CGA)研究主要面向成人，而儿童的持续发育带来了独特的设计考量（如感官敏感性、身体比例、注意力维持等），这些在现有研究中未被充分探索。本研究旨在了解如何为儿科护理设计合适的CGA技术。

Method: 对12名儿科临床医生和1名系统设计师进行定性研究，这些参与者日常使用CGA技术。

Result: 识别出儿童特有的挑战：对可穿戴设备的感官敏感性、传感器放置和校准中的身体比例适应、数据收集期间的患者参与度维持；临床医生还提出了工作流程调整需求，以及将步态分析从实验室扩展到自然场景（如操场和学校）的兴趣。

Conclusion: 提出了以儿科为中心的CGA设计建议，包括适合感官敏感儿童的传感模式，以及在不同护理环境中采集步态数据的方法。研究贡献在于将临床技术适应儿科人群独特需求这一更广泛的挑战。

Abstract: Computerized gait analysis (CGA) serves as an essential diagnostic tool for evaluating neuromuscular, musculoskeletal, and neurological disorders in children, from cerebral palsy to muscular dystrophy. By enabling objective and comprehensive gait analysis, CGA supports timely clinical interventions that can significantly improve pediatric mobility outcomes and quality of life. Yet pediatric gait analysis introduces unique design considerations often underexplored in existing CGA research, as children's ongoing development shapes assessment requirements. To understand how CGA technologies can be designed for pediatric care, we conducted a qualitative study with 12 pediatric clinicians and one system designer who routinely work with CGA. Participants identified child-specific challenges including managing heightened sensory sensitivities to wearable devices, accommodating body proportions in sensor placement and calibration, and maintaining patient engagement during data collection. Clinicians also articulated needs for workflow adaptations and expressed interest in extending gait analysis beyond controlled laboratory settings into naturalistic environments such as playgrounds and schools, where children's authentic movement patterns emerge. Drawing from these clinician perspectives, we present design recommendations for pediatric-centered CGA that address sensing modalities suitable for sensory-sensitive children and approaches for capturing gait data across diverse care environments. Our findings contribute to the broader challenge of adapting clinical technologies to meet the distinct needs of pediatric populations.

</details>


### [308] [BlossomPsy: A User-Centric AI System for Adaptive and Engaging MBTI Personality Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06149)
*Bingjia Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BlossomPsy是一个基于AI的MBTI人格评估系统，通过多轮对话和图片问答增强用户参与度，结合深度学习、多臂老虎机算法和控制理论实现动态自适应评估。实验表明其在保持预测一致性的同时，用户满意度高于传统MBTI量表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人格量表虽然标准但冗长难用，现有AI系统难以平衡用户参与度与预测一致性。本文旨在开发一个既有趣味性又能保持预测稳定性的AI驱动评估系统。

Method: 系统整合了多轮对话和图片问答两种交互形式；采用深度学习进行特征提取，多臂老虎机算法优化问题选择策略，控制理论确保预测的置信度感知和动态稳定性。

Result: 通过人类志愿者和LLM实验，BlossomPsy能够产生稳定的预测结果，相比MBTI-M（中文版）获得了更高的用户满意度，同时与参考量表保持相当的一致性。

Conclusion: 融合多模态交互和自适应算法的AI评估系统可以在提升用户体验的同时不牺牲预测的稳定性和一致性，为人格评估工具的普及提供了可行方案。

Abstract: There has been growing public interest in understanding personality traits and emotional characteristics, as such knowledge helps individuals better accept themselves and manage negative emotions. While professional personality scales remain the standard tool for assessment, they are often perceived as tedious or inaccessible to the general public. AI-driven systems can make assessments more accessible, but it is difficult to balance user engagement with predictive consistency in existing works. We tackle this challenge by introducing BlossomPsy, a user-friendly AI-driven MBTI assessment system. MBTI, a widely recognized but psychometrically debated personality framework, serves as the foundation for many recent systems. BlossomPsy integrates multi-turn dialogue and photo-based questions to enhance user engagement while supporting confidence-aware predictions. By combining deep learning, multi-armed bandit algorithms, and control theory, the system dynamically adapts to users' responses. In particular, photo-based questions are designed to increase interactivity and provide additional user information, thereby improving prediction confidence. Experiments involving both human volunteers and large language models (LLMs) provide preliminary evidence that BlossomPsy can produce stable predictions, with higher reported user satisfaction compared to MBTI-M (Chinese version), while maintaining comparable consistency with the reference scale.

</details>


### [309] [DS-MTNet:Structured Multi-Task EEG Decoding for Human-Machine Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06297)
*Xinjia Yu,Yang Zhou,Jing Yang,Tielin Shi,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出DS-MTNet，一种结构化多任务EEG解码框架，通过分解源和多任务网络将操作者的神经信号（事件处理、响应准备、内部状态）统一编码为可重用的源槽表示，在持续注意驾驶数据集上超越多种基线模型，为人机协作中融入操作者神经证据提供了计算基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作系统主要依赖环境传感器观察可见动作和场景状态，未能充分将操作者的内部感知、意图相关和状态相关的神经过程整合到机器感知中。EEG虽能捕获这些神经活动，但现有解码方法通常针对特定任务分类或聚合预测，缺乏将多种HMC相关读出组织为统一EEG表示的框架。

Method: 提出了分解源多任务网络（DS-MTNet），包含三个流：EEG波形、任务路由的源嵌入、时频谱功率特征；将这些特征组织为可重用槽；使用双门控机制路由任务特定成分。在持续注意驾驶EEG数据集上测试三个读出任务：车道偏离相关epoch（环境事件处理）、转向响应阶段（响应准备）、反应时定义的警觉状态（内部状态）。

Result: DS-MTNet在传统方法、单任务深度模型和多任务EEG基线中取得了最佳平均性能，在转向响应阶段解码任务上增益最为显著。消融和可解释性分析表明DS-MTNet能联合解码多个读出任务，并将事件相关、响应相关和状态相关的EEG证据组织在统一的源槽表示中。

Conclusion: 这些发现为将操作者的神经证据融入人机协作中的机器感知提供了计算步骤，展示了利用EEG作为额外传感通道来增强人机协作系统的可行性。

Abstract: Current human-machine collaboration (HMC) systems rely on environment-facing sensors to observe visible actions and scene states, but the internal perceptual, intention-related, and state-related processes of operators remain insufficiently integrated into machine perception. Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive, time-resolved modality to capture neural activity associated with these processes and can serve as an additional sensing channel in HMC. However, HMC-relevant EEG evidence is often mixed in continuous recordings. Existing EEG decoding methods usually target task-specific classification or aggregate prediction, so multiple HMC-relevant readouts are rarely organized in a unified EEG representation. To address this gap, this paper proposed the Decomposed-Source Multi-Task Network (DS-MTNet), a structured multi-task EEG decoding framework. DS-MTNet integrated three streams, namely EEG waveforms, task-routed source embeddings, and temporal-spectral power features, into reusable slots and used dual gating mechanisms to route task-specific components. The model was tested on a sustained-attention driving EEG dataset with three representative readouts: lane-departure-related epochs for environmental-event processing, steering-response stage for response preparation, and reaction-time-defined alertness state for internal state. DS-MTNet achieved the best mean performance among traditional, single-task deep, and multi-task EEG baselines, with the most robust gains observed for steering-response stage decoding. Ablation and interpretability analyses suggested that DS-MTNet jointly decoded multiple readouts and organized event-related, response-related, and state-related EEG evidence in a unified source-slot representation. These findings provide a computational step toward incorporating operator-related neural evidence into machine perception in HMC.

</details>


### [310] [The Impact of Security and Privacy Controls on Users' Emotional Engagement with Generative AI Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06371)
*Jabari Kwesi,Jiaxun Cao,Hailee Cunningham,Pardis Emami-Naeini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过354名美国参与者的混合方法研究，发现生成式AI聊天机器人的安全与隐私控制（尤其是删除功能）比技术性更强的控制（如本地处理、模型训练退出）更能提升用户的情感支持意愿，但用户对控制机制的理解有限且对隐私承诺持怀疑态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式AI聊天机器人中的安全与隐私控制如何影响用户对于情感支持场景下的敏感信息披露意愿、保护感知和有效性感知，填补现有文献对此类控制在情感支持场景下影响的空白。

Method: 采用混合方法的小插曲研究，招募354名美国参与者，通过情境实验考察不同S&P控制（删除功能、本地处理、模型训练退出）对用户情感支持意愿、保护感知和有效性感知的影响。

Result: 删除披露内容的控制对用户意愿影响最大，效果优于本地处理等更复杂的技术控制；参与者对复杂机制的理解存在困难；用户对隐私控制的实际功能持怀疑态度，信任脆弱。

Conclusion: 提出行动建议：弥合用户理解差距、建立可信保证、适当校准对处于困扰状态用户的障碍设计。

Abstract: Chatbots powered by generative AI (e.g., OpenAI's ChatGPT and Google's Gemini) are increasingly being appropriated for emotional support and companionship. These tools offer a suite of security and privacy (S&P) controls, including model training opt-outs and memory toggles, yet how the presence of these controls influences users' attitudes toward emotionally sensitive disclosure remains understudied. We conducted a mixed-methods vignette study with 354 U.S. participants to examine how S&P controls influence users' willingness to engage with generative AI chatbots for emotional support, their perceptions of how protected they are when using these systems, and their perceptions of how effective the chatbots are for providing support. Controls enabling deletion of disclosures had the largest positive impact: these offerings outperformed technically sophisticated controls such as local-only processing and model training opt-outs, where participants expressed difficulty understanding the underlying mechanisms. Yet trust remains fragile, and participants often doubted S&P controls would function as promised. We conclude with actionable recommendations informed by our results to bridge users' comprehension gaps, build credible assurances, and properly calibrate barriers for users in distress.

</details>


### [311] [GlassTENG: Self-Powered Triboelectric Nanogenerator based Sensing of Pulse, Jaw, and Upper Facial Activity from Everyday Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06509)
*Raj N. Dave,Jovanis Prodanich,Yung-ching Lai,Oscar Jakacki,Stanley Lin,Jack Thoene,Nabil Alshurafa,Nivedita Arora*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出GlassTENG，一种将三个定制摩擦纳米发电机嵌入眼镜框架的超低功耗生理传感平台，能够在自供电条件下同时采集动脉脉搏、下颌运动和上面部活动信号，在20人用户研究中实现93.8%的活动识别准确率和1.82 BPM的心率估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜虽具有持续皮肤接触的优势，但长期佩戴面临传感器-皮肤接触 discomfort（如凝胶和粘合剂）以及传感器功耗导致电池体积、重量和维护负担增加的问题，使得纵向部署在量化自我场景中不切实际。

Method: 将三个定制摩擦纳米发电机（TENG）嵌入眼镜框架，分别放置在鼻梁处的角动脉、延长臂上的颞浅动脉和太阳穴处的颞肌位置。每个传感器在模拟前端仅消耗1.36 μW功率，通过自供电方式将机械能转换为电能。

Result: 在20人用户研究中，对六种下颌和上面部活动实现了93.8%的分类准确率；在30秒窗口内，相对于地面真相胸带传感器，心率估计的平均绝对误差为1.82 BPM。

Conclusion: 该工作为长期佩戴、超低功耗、基于眼镜的生理监测平台指明了未来发展方向。

Abstract: Smart glasses maintain near-continuous skin contact at multiple arterial and muscular sites, making them a promising platform for physiological sensing. In practice, though, two factors make sustained daily wear and longitudinal deployment impractical for the quantified self: the discomfort of prolonged sensor-skin contact (e.g., gels and adhesives) and the sensor power demands that increase battery size, weight, and maintenance burden. We present GlassTENG, an ultra-low-power sensor that embeds three custom-fabricated triboelectric nanogenerators (TENGs) into a glasses frame at the angular artery on the nasal bridge, the superficial temporal artery on an extended arm, and the temporalis muscle at the temple. Each GlassTENG sensor is self-powered in transducing mechanical energy to electrical energy and consumes 1.36 $μ$W per sensor at the analog front-end. GlassTENG enables simultaneous capture of arterial pulse waveforms, jaw kinematics (e.g., clenching, tapping, eating), and upper facial activity (e.g., blinking, eyebrow movement). In a 20-participant user study, we achieve 93.8% accuracy across six jaw and upper facial activities and estimate heart rate with a mean absolute error of 1.82 beats per minute (BPM) relative to a ground-truth chest-strap sensor in 30s windows. Together, these results establish a future pathway toward a longitudinally worn, ultra-low-power, glasses-based physiological monitoring platform.

</details>
